 * IMPORTANT: this module intentionally does NOT register any input-router
 * gesture handler and does NOT listen to middle-mouse or wheel. The canonical
 * Revit navigation in src/features/navigation.ts is the SOLE owner of:
 *   MMB drag = pan, Shift/Ctrl+MMB = orbit, Wheel = zoom-to-cursor.
 *
 * Duplicating those here was the cause of "view rotates / zooms out on MMB
 * release": two handlers fought over the same gesture and the losing one
 * applied a stale orbit/recenter when the gesture ended.
 *
 * All this file does now: after a LEFT-click selection settles, re-center the
 * orbit pivot on the current selection (the documented "focus on select"
 * behaviour), using camera-tools helpers via the global context. No MMB, no
 * wheel, no drag.
 */

let installed = false;

function camTools(): Any {
  const c = ctx as Any;
  return (c.cam as Any) ?? (window as Any).__OBS_CAMERA_TOOLS ?? {};
}

// Debounce so rapid left-clicks through a coordination model do not queue
// dozens of overlapping Fragments worker round-trips (getElementWorldBoxes
// is async and slow on large models).
let pendingPivot: number | null = null;

function schedulePivotOnSelection(): void {
  if (pendingPivot !== null) {
    window.clearTimeout(pendingPivot);
  }
  pendingPivot = window.setTimeout(() => {
    pendingPivot = null;
    pivotOnSelectionSafe();
  }, 40);
}

function pivotOnSelectionSafe(): void {
  try {
    const t = camTools();
    if (typeof t.pivotOnSelection === "function") {
      void t.pivotOnSelection();
    }
  } catch {
    /* camera-tools not ready yet; ignore */
  }
}

function setPivotSafe(point: Any): void {
  try {
    const t = camTools();
    if (typeof t.setOrbitPivot === "function") {
      t.setOrbitPivot(point);
    }
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
}

export function setupSelectionOrbit(): void {
  if (installed) return;
  installed = true;

  // LEFT click only -> recenter orbit pivot on the resulting selection.
  const onClick = (e: MouseEvent): void => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;            // never react to MMB / RMB
    const tgt = e.target as Any;
    if (tgt && typeof tgt.closest === "function" &&
        tgt.closest('button, input, select, textarea, [contenteditable="true"], .obs-ribbon, .obs-panel, .obs-property, .obs-dialog, .obs-adv-toolbar')) {
      return;                              // ignore UI chrome clicks
    }
    // let the highlighter / selection commit first, then recenter
    schedulePivotOnSelection();
  };

  window.addEventListener("click", onClick, true);

  // Explicit focus requests from other modules (e.g. "Zoom to Selection").
  window.addEventListener("obs:focus-selection", () => pivotOnSelectionSafe());
  window.addEventListener("obs:selection-changed", () => {
    // soft recenter only when something is actually selected
    window.setTimeout(pivotOnSelectionSafe, 60);
  });

  (window as Any).__obsSelectionOrbit = {
    enabled: true,
    setPivot: setPivotSafe,
    pivotOnSelection: pivotOnSelectionSafe,
  };

  console.log(
    "[selection-orbit] thin mode active: pivot-on-left-click only; " +
    "MMB pan/orbit and wheel zoom are owned exclusively by navigation.ts",
  );
}

// Compatibility aliases (in case any importer used a different entry name).
export function installSelectionOrbit(): void { setupSelectionOrbit(); }
export function setupOrbit(): void { setupSelectionOrbit(); }
export function enableSelectionOrbit(): void { /* no-op, always on */ }
export function disableSelectionOrbit(): void { /* no-op */ }
export function isSelectionOrbitEnabled(): boolean { return true; }

===== FILE: src/features/navigation.ts =====
// openbim-fix:v3:applied -- do not re-apply the earlier fix scripts
/**
 * Revit-class 3D Navigation (reset / hardened)
 *
 * Canonical Revit mouse map:
 *   MMB drag              Pan
 *   Shift + MMB drag      Orbit (around target)
 *   Ctrl + MMB drag       Orbit around selection
 *   Wheel                 Zoom to cursor
 *   Shift + Wheel         Zoom x3
 *   Double-click MMB      Zoom to fit
 *   LMB click             Select
 *   LMB drag L->R         Window select
 *   LMB drag R->L         Crossing select
 *   RMB                   Context menu
 *
 * CameraControls mouse/touch are forced OFF so only this module drives the view.
 * Every gesture ends with forceResumeCameraControls to prevent 3D hang-ups.
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";
import { inputRouter, type Gesture, type Mods } from "../core/input-router";
import { fitAll, zoomBy, pickWorldPointUnderCursor } from "./camera-tools";
import { forceResumeCameraControls } from "../core/camera-gate";

type NavState =
  | { kind: "idle" }
  | {
      kind: "pan";
      startX: number;
      startY: number;
      startTarget: THREE.Vector3;
      startCam: THREE.Vector3;
      right: THREE.Vector3;
      up: THREE.Vector3;
      worldPerPx: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "orbit";
      startX: number;
      startY: number;
      pivot: THREE.Vector3;
      startSpherical: THREE.Spherical;
      pixelsToRadians: number;
    }
  | {
      kind: "box-select";
      startX: number;
      startY: number;
      mode: "window" | "crossing";
    };

let state: NavState = { kind: "idle" };
let shiftHeld = false;
let ctrlHeld = false;

const blockers: Array<() => boolean> = [];

export function whenNavigationBlocked(fn: () => boolean): () => void {
  blockers.push(fn);
  return () => {
    const i = blockers.indexOf(fn);
    if (i >= 0) blockers.splice(i, 1);
  };
}

function blocked(): boolean {
  for (const b of blockers) {
    try {
      if (b()) return true;
    } catch {
      /* */
    }
  }
  try {
    if ((window as any).__obsPlanVisible?.()) return true;
    if (ctx.features?.drawing?.isActive?.()) return true;
    if (ctx.features?.walkMode?.isEnabled?.()) return true;
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  return false;
}

export type ClickDelegate = (m: Mods, e: PointerEvent) => boolean;
export type DragDelegate = (g: Extract<Gesture, { kind: "drag-move" }>) => boolean;

const clickDelegates: ClickDelegate[] = [];
const dragDelegates: DragDelegate[] = [];

export function onNavClick(fn: ClickDelegate): () => void {
  clickDelegates.push(fn);
  return () => {
    const i = clickDelegates.indexOf(fn);
    if (i >= 0) clickDelegates.splice(i, 1);
  };
}

export function onNavDrag(fn: DragDelegate): () => void {
  dragDelegates.push(fn);
  return () => {
    const i = dragDelegates.indexOf(fn);
    if (i >= 0) dragDelegates.splice(i, 1);
  };
}

function controls(): any {
  return ctx.world.camera.controls;
}

function camera(): THREE.PerspectiveCamera | THREE.OrthographicCamera {
  return ctx.world.camera.three as any;
}

function getTarget(out = new THREE.Vector3()): THREE.Vector3 {
  const c = controls();
  if (c?.target?.isVector3) return out.copy(c.target);
  return out.set(0, 0, 0);
}

let cachedSelectionCenter: THREE.Vector3 | null = null;
let selectionCenterToken = 0;

async function refreshSelectionCenterCache(): Promise<void> {
  const myToken = ++selectionCenterToken;
  try {
    const box = await (ctx.features as any)?.selection?.boundingBox?.();
    if (myToken !== selectionCenterToken) return;
    if (box && !box.isEmpty()) {
      cachedSelectionCenter = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
      return;
    }
  } catch { /* */ }
  if (myToken !== selectionCenterToken) return;
  cachedSelectionCenter = null;
}

function getOrbitPivot(): THREE.Vector3 {
  if (cachedSelectionCenter) return cachedSelectionCenter.clone();
  return getTarget();
}

function beginPan(e: PointerEvent): void {
  const cam = camera();
  const rect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
  if (rect.height < 1) return;

  const target = getTarget();
  const dist = cam.position.distanceTo(target);
  const isOrtho = (cam as any).isOrthographicCamera;
  let worldPerPx: number;
  if (isOrtho) {
    // In ortho, world-per-pixel depends on the frustum height and the
    // current zoom factor: (top - bottom) / zoom / viewportHeight.
    const o = cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera;
    const z = Math.max(1e-3, o.zoom || 1);
    worldPerPx = ((o.top - o.bottom) / z) / rect.height;
  } else {
    const vFov = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad((cam as THREE.PerspectiveCamera).fov ?? 60);
    worldPerPx = (2 * Math.tan(vFov / 2) * Math.max(dist, 0.01)) / rect.height;
  }

  const right = new THREE.Vector3().setFromMatrixColumn(cam.matrixWorld, 0);
  const up = new THREE.Vector3().setFromMatrixColumn(cam.matrixWorld, 1);

  state = {
    kind: "pan",
    startX: e.clientX,
    startY: e.clientY,
    startTarget: target.clone(),
    startCam: cam.position.clone(),
    right,
    up,
    worldPerPx,
  };
}

function applyPan(dxTotal: number, dyTotal: number): void {
  if (state.kind !== "pan") return;
  const cam = camera();
  const c = controls();

  const offset = state.right
    .clone()
    .multiplyScalar(-dxTotal * state.worldPerPx)
    .add(state.up.clone().multiplyScalar(dyTotal * state.worldPerPx));

  const newPos = state.startCam.clone().add(offset);
  const newTarget = state.startTarget.clone().add(offset);

  try {
    if (c && c.enabled === false) c.enabled = true;
  } catch { /* */ }

  try {
    c.setLookAt(newPos.x, newPos.y, newPos.z, newTarget.x, newTarget.y, newTarget.z, false);
    try { c.update?.(0); } catch { /* */ }
  } catch {
    cam.position.copy(newPos);
    try { cam.lookAt(newTarget); } catch { /* */ }
  }
  try { ctx.cam.updateClipping(); } catch { /* */ }
}

function beginOrbitSync(e: PointerEvent, pivot: THREE.Vector3): void {
  const cam = camera();
  const camToPivot = cam.position.clone().sub(pivot);
  const spherical = new THREE.Spherical().setFromVector3(camToPivot);
  const rect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
  const pixelsToRadians = (Math.PI * 2) / Math.max(rect.height, 400);

  state = {
    kind: "orbit",
    startX: e.clientX,
    startY: e.clientY,
    pivot,
    startSpherical: spherical,
    pixelsToRadians,
  };
}

async function selectionCenter(): Promise<THREE.Vector3> {
  try {
    const box = await (ctx.features as any)?.selection?.boundingBox?.();
    if (box && !box.isEmpty()) return box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  const auth = (ctx.features as any)?.authoring;
  if (auth?.getSelectedIds?.()?.length) {
    const ids = auth.getSelectedIds() as string[];
    const b = new THREE.Box3();
    for (const id of ids) {
      const el = auth.getElementById?.(id);
      if (!el) continue;
      const wb = auth.getElementWorldBox?.(el);
      if (wb && !wb.isEmpty()) b.union(wb);
    }
    if (!b.isEmpty()) return b.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  }
  return getTarget();
}

function applyOrbit(dxTotal: number, dyTotal: number): void {
  if (state.kind !== "orbit") return;
  const cam = camera();
  const c = controls();

  const sph = state.startSpherical.clone();
  sph.theta -= dxTotal * state.pixelsToRadians * 1.4;
  sph.phi -= dyTotal * state.pixelsToRadians * 1.4;
  sph.phi = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(sph.phi, 0.02, Math.PI - 0.02);

  // In ortho, the visible size is controlled by zoom, so any radius
  // works â€” but keep it close for stable near/far clipping.
  const isOrtho = (cam as any).isOrthographicCamera;
  if (isOrtho) {
    sph.radius = Math.max(sph.radius, __obsGetSceneRadiusFallback() * 1.5);
  }

  const newPos = state.pivot.clone().add(new THREE.Vector3().setFromSpherical(sph));

  try { if (c && c.enabled === false) c.enabled = true; } catch { /* */ }

  try {
    c.setLookAt(newPos.x, newPos.y, newPos.z, state.pivot.x, state.pivot.y, state.pivot.z, false);
    try { c.update?.(0); } catch { /* */ }
  } catch {
    cam.position.copy(newPos);
    cam.lookAt(state.pivot);
  }
  try { ctx.cam.updateClipping(); } catch { /* */ }
}

export function zoomAtCursor(deltaY: number, mods: Mods, focus: THREE.Vector3 | null): void {
  const multiplier = mods.shift ? 2.5 : 1;
  const discrete = mods.ctrl || mods.meta ? Math.sign(deltaY || 1) * 0.2 : 0;
  const raw = (deltaY || 0) * 0.0012 * multiplier;
  let factor = Math.exp(raw + discrete);
  if (!Number.isFinite(factor) || factor <= 0) return;
  if (Math.abs(factor - 1) < 0.002) return;
  zoomBy(factor, focus ?? undefined);
}

let overlay: HTMLDivElement | null = null;

function ensureOverlay(): HTMLDivElement {
  if (overlay) return overlay;
  overlay = document.createElement("div");
  overlay.id = "obs-nav-box";
  overlay.style.cssText =
    "position:absolute;pointer-events:none;z-index:70;display:none;" +
    "box-sizing:border-box;border-radius:1px";
  ctx.rightHost.appendChild(overlay);
  return overlay;
}

function renderBox(
  mode: "window" | "crossing",
  ax: number,
  ay: number,
  bx: number,
  by: number,
): void {
  const el = ensureOverlay();
  const x1 = Math.min(ax, bx);
  const y1 = Math.min(ay, by);
  const x2 = Math.max(ax, bx);
  const y2 = Math.max(ay, by);
  el.style.left = `${x1}px`;
  el.style.top = `${y1}px`;
  el.style.width = `${Math.max(1, x2 - x1)}px`;
  el.style.height = `${Math.max(1, y2 - y1)}px`;
  el.style.display = "block";
  if (mode === "window") {
    el.style.background = "rgba(56,189,248,0.14)";
    el.style.border = "1px solid #38bdf8";
  } else {
    el.style.background = "rgba(34,197,94,0.14)";
    el.style.border = "1px dashed #22c55e";
  }
}

function hideBox(): void {
  if (overlay) overlay.style.display = "none";
}

function attachModifierTracking(): void {
  window.addEventListener(
    "keydown",
    (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Shift") shiftHeld = true;
      else if (e.key === "Control" || e.key === "Meta") ctrlHeld = true;
    },
    true,
  );
  window.addEventListener(
    "keyup",
    (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Shift") shiftHeld = false;
      else if (e.key === "Control" || e.key === "Meta") ctrlHeld = false;
    },
    true,
  );
  window.addEventListener("blur", () => {
    shiftHeld = false;
    ctrlHeld = false;
  });
}

function isUiTarget(e: Event): boolean {
  const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
  if (!t || !t.closest) return false;
  if (t.tagName === "CANVAS") return false;
  return !!t.closest(
    "input,select,textarea,button,label,a,[contenteditable]," +
      ".obs-revit-options,.obs-revit-contextual,.obs-revit-viewbar," +
      ".obs-plan-overlay,#obs-ai-chat,#obs-command-palette,#obs-viewcube," +
      "[data-obs-panel],.obs-panel,.bb-status,.obs-toast-host",
  );
}

export function setupNavigation(): void {
  ensureOverlay();
  attachModifierTracking();
  disableNativeCameraMouse();
  try { forceResumeCameraControls(); } catch { /* */ }

  try {
    const sel: any = (ctx.features as any).selection;
    sel?.onChange?.(() => { void refreshSelectionCenterCache(); });
    void refreshSelectionCenterCache();
  } catch { /* */ }

  const safetyEnd = () => {
    endNavGesture();
  };
  window.addEventListener("pointercancel", safetyEnd, true);
  window.addEventListener("blur", safetyEnd);
  document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", () => {
    if (document.hidden) safetyEnd();
  });

  inputRouter.registerCancel(() => {
    if (state.kind === "idle") return false;
    endNavGesture();
    return true;
  });

  inputRouter.subscribe((g) => {
    if (g.kind === "drag-start" && g.button === 1) {
      if (blocked() || isUiTarget(g.event)) return false;
      disableNativeCameraMouse();
      try { forceResumeCameraControls(); } catch { /* */ }
      const wantOrbit = shiftHeld || ctrlHeld || g.mods.shift || g.mods.ctrl || g.mods.meta;
      if (wantOrbit) {
        const pivot = getOrbitPivot();
        beginOrbitSync(g.event, pivot);
        ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "grabbing";
        if (!cachedSelectionCenter) {
          void selectionCenter().then((p) => {
            if (state.kind !== "orbit") return;
            if (p.distanceTo(state.pivot) < 1e-3) return;
            beginOrbitSync(g.event, p);
          });
        }
      } else {
        beginPan(g.event);
        ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "move";
      }
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-move" && g.button === 1) {
      const wantOrbit = shiftHeld || g.mods.shift || ctrlHeld || g.mods.ctrl || g.mods.meta;
      if (wantOrbit && state.kind === "pan") {
        beginOrbitSync(g.event, getOrbitPivot());
        ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "grabbing";
      }
      if (state.kind === "pan") {
        applyPan(g.dx, g.dy);
        return true;
      }
      if (state.kind === "orbit") {
        applyOrbit(g.dx, g.dy);
        return true;
      }
      return false;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-end" && g.button === 1) {
      if (state.kind === "pan" || state.kind === "orbit") {
        endNavGesture();
        return true;
      }
      try { forceResumeCameraControls(); } catch { /* */ }
      return false;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-start" && g.button === 0) {
      if (blocked() || isUiTarget(g.event)) return false;
      const probe: Extract<Gesture, { kind: "drag-move" }> = {
        kind: "drag-move",
        button: 0,
        mods: g.mods,
        dx: 0,
        dy: 0,
        event: g.event,
      };
      for (const d of dragDelegates) {
        try {
          if (d(probe)) return false;
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("[navigation] drag delegate threw", err);
        }
      }
      state = {
        kind: "box-select",
        startX: g.event.clientX,
        startY: g.event.clientY,
        mode: "window",
      };
      ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "crosshair";
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-move" && state.kind === "box-select") {
      for (const d of dragDelegates) {
        try {
          if (d(g)) {
            hideBox();
            state = { kind: "idle" };
            ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = "";
            return false;
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("[navigation] drag delegate threw", err);
        }
      }
      const rect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
      const mode: "window" | "crossing" =
        g.event.clientX >= (state as any).startX ? "window" : "crossing";
      (state as any).mode = mode;
      renderBox(
        mode,
        (state as any).startX - rect.left,
        (state as any).startY - rect.top,
        g.event.clientX - rect.left,
        g.event.clientY - rect.top,
      );
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-end" && g.button === 0 && state.kind === "box-select") {
      const rect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x1 = state.startX - rect.left;
      const y1 = state.startY - rect.top;
      const x2 = g.event.clientX - rect.left;
      const y2 = g.event.clientY - rect.top;
      hideBox();
      state = { kind: "idle" };
      try { ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = ""; } catch { /* */ }
      try { forceResumeCameraControls(); } catch { /* */ }

      if (Math.abs(x2 - x1) < 3 && Math.abs(y2 - y1) < 3) return false;

      const mode: "window" | "crossing" = x2 >= x1 ? "window" : "crossing";
      void (ctx.features as any).boxSelection?.commit?.(
        {
          x1: Math.min(x1, x2),
          y1: Math.min(y1, y2),
          x2: Math.max(x1, x2),
          y2: Math.max(y1, y2),
        },
        mode,
      );
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "click" && g.button === 0) {
      if (blocked()) return false;
      for (const d of clickDelegates) {
        try {
          if (d(g.mods, g.event)) return true;
        } catch (err) {
          console.error("[navigation] click delegate threw", err);
        }
      }
      return false;
    }

    if (g.kind === "wheel") {
      if ((window as any).__OBS_MMB_HELD) return true;
      if (blocked() || isUiTarget(g.event)) return false;
      const mods: Mods = g.mods ?? {
        ctrl: g.event.ctrlKey,
        shift: g.event.shiftKey,
        alt: g.event.altKey,
        meta: g.event.metaKey,
      };
      // openbim-fix: navigation.ts -- check wheel authority before raycasting
      if ((window as any).__OBS_WHEEL_AUTHORITY) return false;
      const focus = pickWorldPointUnderCursor(g.event.clientX, g.event.clientY);
      zoomAtCursor(g.event.deltaY, mods, focus);
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "dblclick" && g.button === 1) {
      if (blocked() || isUiTarget(g.event)) return false;
      fitAll();
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }, 30);

  ctx.rightHost.addEventListener(
    "wheel",
    (e) => {
      if (!isUiTarget(e)) e.preventDefault();
    },
    { passive: false },
  );

  (ctx.features as any).navigation = {
    getState: () => state,
    whenBlocked: whenNavigationBlocked,
    onNavClick,
    onNavDrag,
  };

  console.log("[navigation] Revit-style navigation active");
}

export function getNavigationState(): NavState {
  return state;
}

export function cancelNavigationGesture(): void {
  state = { kind: "idle" };
  hideBox();
  try { ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = ""; } catch { /* */ }
  try { forceResumeCameraControls(); } catch { /* */ }
}

function disableNativeCameraMouse(): void {
  try {
    const c: any = controls();
    if (!c) return;
    const ACTION = c.constructor?.ACTION || (window as any).CameraControls?.ACTION;
    if (c.mouseButtons && ACTION) {
      c.mouseButtons.left = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
      c.mouseButtons.middle = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
      c.mouseButtons.right = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
      c.mouseButtons.wheel = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
    }
    if (c.touches && ACTION) {
      try {
        c.touches.one = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        c.touches.two = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        c.touches.three = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
      } catch { /* */ }
    }
    /* dampingFactor deprecated; smoothTime is used instead */
    try { if ("draggingSmoothTime" in c) c.draggingSmoothTime = 0; } catch { /* */ }
    try { if ("azimuthRotateSpeed" in c) c.azimuthRotateSpeed = 1; } catch { /* */ }
    try { if ("polarRotateSpeed" in c) c.polarRotateSpeed = 1; } catch { /* */ }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[navigation] disableNativeCameraMouse", e);
  }
}

function endNavGesture(resetCursor = true): void {
  state = { kind: "idle" };
  hideBox();
  if (resetCursor) {
    try { ctx.rightHost.style.cursor = ""; } catch { /* */ }
  }
  try { forceResumeCameraControls(); } catch { /* */ }
}

function __obsGetSceneRadiusFallback(): number {
  try {
    const c: any = (window as any).__OBS_CTX__;
    const box = c?.scene?.box;

    if (box && typeof box.isEmpty === "function" && !box.isEmpty()) {
      const min = box.min;
      const max = box.max;

      if (min && max) {
        const dx = Number(max.x) - Number(min.x);
        const dy = Number(max.y) - Number(min.y);
        const dz = Number(max.z) - Number(min.z);

        const r = Math.hypot(dx, dy, dz);

        if (Number.isFinite(r) && r > 0) {
          return r;
        }
      }
    }
  } catch {
    // Ignore and use fallback radius.
  }

  return 100;
}


===== FILE: src/features/camera-tools.ts =====
import * as THREE from "three";
import type * as OBC from "@thatopen/components";
import { ctx, type Projection } from "../core/context";
import { forceResumeCameraControls } from "../core/camera-gate";
import { register, type CameraService } from "../core/services";
import { getElementWorldBoxes } from "../modules/geometry/world-box";

// â”€â”€ Scene placement state â”€â”€
let recenterEnabled = false;
let sceneOffset: THREE.Vector3 | null = null;
let offsetEstablished = false;
const sceneCenter = new THREE.Vector3();
let sceneRadius = 10;
const lastSceneBox = new THREE.Box3();

export const boundsInfoEl = document.createElement("div");
boundsInfoEl.style.cssText = "font-size:10.5px;opacity:.7;margin-top:6px;line-height:1.5";
boundsInfoEl.textContent = "Scene: empty";

const boundsHelperGroup = new THREE.Group();
let showBounds = false;

export function setRecenterEnabled(on: boolean) {
  recenterEnabled = on;
}

export function resetPlacementState() {
  sceneOffset = null;
  offsetEstablished = false;
}

function worldReady(): boolean {
  try {
    const w: any = (ctx as any).world;
    return !!w && !!w.camera && !!w.camera.three;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function isOrthoCam(cam: THREE.Camera): boolean {
  return !!(cam as any).isOrthographicCamera;
}

function allCameras(): THREE.Camera[] {
  if (!worldReady()) return [];
  const c: any = ctx.world.camera;
  const out: any[] = [ctx.world.camera.three];
  const push = (x: any) => {
    if (!x) return;
    if (x.isCamera) out.push(x);
    else if (x.three?.isCamera) out.push(x.three);
  };
  push(c.perspectiveCamera);
  push(c.orthoCamera);
  push(c.orthographicCamera);
  return Array.from(new Set(out)).filter((x) => x && x.isCamera);
}

/**
 * Ensure the frustum of any orthographic camera is large enough to contain
 * the scene. This is what makes every camera-move work correctly in ortho.
 */
function fitOrthoFrustum(ortho: THREE.OrthographicCamera, radius: number): void {
  // Aspect must come from the viewport, not the current (possibly stale) frustum.
  let aspect = 1;
  try {
    const host = ctx.rightHost;
    if (host) {
      const r = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (r.width > 0 && r.height > 0) aspect = r.width / r.height;
    }
  } catch { /* */ }
  const safeAspect = Number.isFinite(aspect) && aspect > 0.01 ? aspect : 1;
  const safeR = Math.max(radius, 0.5);
  const halfH = safeR * 1.15;
  const halfW = halfH * safeAspect;
  ortho.left = -halfW;
  ortho.right = halfW;
  ortho.top = halfH;
  ortho.bottom = -halfH;
  ortho.zoom = 1;
  ortho.near = -safeR * 50;
  ortho.far = safeR * 200 + 1000;
  ortho.updateProjectionMatrix();
}

/** Orthographic cameras use near/far differently â€” set generously. */
function applyClipping(near: number, far: number) {
  for (const cam of allCameras()) {
    if (isOrthoCam(cam)) {
      let ortho = cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera;
    if (!(ortho as any).isOrthographicCamera) {
      const _c: any = ctx.world.camera;
      const _cands = [_c.orthographicCamera, _c.orthoCamera];
      for (const c of _cands) {
        const t: any = c?.three ?? c;
        if (t?.isOrthographicCamera) { ortho = t; break; }
      }
    }
      const safeFar = Math.max(Math.abs(far), sceneRadius * 4, 2000);
      let changed = false;
      // Ortho can use a negative near plane safely â€” this eliminates
      // z-fighting on flat models and keeps geometry behind the eye visible.
      const nearTarget = -safeFar;
      if (Math.abs(ortho.near - nearTarget) > 1e-3) {
        ortho.near = nearTarget;
        changed = true;
      }
      if (Math.abs(ortho.far - safeFar) > 1e-3) {
        ortho.far = safeFar;
        changed = true;
      }
      if (changed) ortho.updateProjectionMatrix();
    } else {
      const c = cam as THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
      let changed = false;
      if (Math.abs(c.near - near) > 1e-6) { c.near = near; changed = true; }
      if (Math.abs(c.far - far) > 1e-6) { c.far = far; changed = true; }
      if (changed) c.updateProjectionMatrix();
    }
  }
}

export function updateClipping() {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const dist = Math.max(cam.position.distanceTo(sceneCenter), 0.05);
  const near = Math.max(0.005, (dist - sceneRadius) / 100);
  const far = (dist + sceneRadius) * 4 + 100;
  applyClipping(near, far);
}

function refreshBoundsInfo() {
  const size = new THREE.Vector3();
  lastSceneBox.getSize(size);
  const active: any = worldReady() ? ctx.world.camera.three : null;
  const isOrtho = active && isOrthoCam(active);
  const clip = isOrtho
    ? `Ortho zoom ${active.zoom?.toFixed(2) ?? "?"}`
    : `near ${active?.near?.toFixed(3) ?? "?"} / far ${active?.far?.toFixed(0) ?? "?"}`;
  boundsInfoEl.innerHTML = lastSceneBox.isEmpty()
    ? "Scene: empty"
    : `Scene: <b>${size.x.toFixed(1)} Ã— ${size.y.toFixed(1)} Ã— ${size.z.toFixed(1)} m</b><br>` +
      `Center: ${sceneCenter.x.toFixed(1)}, ${sceneCenter.y.toFixed(1)}, ${sceneCenter.z.toFixed(1)}<br>` +
      `Radius: ${sceneRadius.toFixed(1)} m Â· ${clip}`;
}

export function computeSceneBounds() {
  lastSceneBox.makeEmpty();
  try {
    for (const [, model] of ctx.fragments.list) {
      lastSceneBox.union(new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(model.object));
    }
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  try {
    const ab = (ctx.features as any).fileInterop?.getAuxBox?.();
    if (ab && !ab.isEmpty()) lastSceneBox.union(ab);
  } catch {
    /* */
  }

  if (lastSceneBox.isEmpty()) {
    sceneCenter.set(0, 0, 0);
    sceneRadius = 10;
  } else {
    lastSceneBox.getCenter(sceneCenter);
    sceneRadius = Math.max(
      lastSceneBox.getBoundingSphere(new THREE.Sphere()).radius,
      0.5,
    );
  }
  updateClipping();
  try {
    (ctx.world.camera.controls as any).maxDistance = Math.max(
      sceneRadius * 100,
      1000,
    );
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  refreshBoundsInfo();
  refreshBoundsHelpers();
}

function refreshBoundsHelpers() {
  while (boundsHelperGroup.children.length) {
    const ch = boundsHelperGroup.children[0] as any;
    boundsHelperGroup.remove(ch);
    ch.geometry?.dispose?.();
    ch.material?.dispose?.();
  }
  if (!showBounds) return;
  if (!lastSceneBox.isEmpty()) {
    boundsHelperGroup.add(
      new THREE.Box3Helper(lastSceneBox.clone(), new THREE.Color(0x22d3ee)),
    );
  }
  try {
    for (const [, model] of ctx.fragments.list) {
      const b = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(model.object);
      if (!b.isEmpty()) {
        boundsHelperGroup.add(new THREE.Box3Helper(b, new THREE.Color(0xf59e0b)));
      }
    }
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
}

export function showBoundsHelpers(on: boolean) {
  showBounds = on;
  refreshBoundsHelpers();
}

function applySceneOffsetTo(modelId: string) {
  const model = ctx.fragments.list.get(modelId);
  if (!model) return;
  const off = sceneOffset ?? new THREE.Vector3();
  model.object.position.set(-off.x, -off.y, -off.z);
}

export function establishSceneOffset(modelId: string) {
  const model = ctx.fragments.list.get(modelId);
  if (!model) return;
  if (!recenterEnabled) return;
  if (!offsetEstablished) {
    offsetEstablished = true;
    const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(model.object);
    sceneOffset = box.isEmpty()
      ? new THREE.Vector3()
      : box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  }
  applySceneOffsetTo(modelId);
}

export function alignOriginToOrigin() {
  try {
    sceneOffset = new THREE.Vector3();
    offsetEstablished = true;
    for (const id of Array.from(ctx.fragments.list.keys())) {
      const model = ctx.fragments.list.get(id);
      if (!model) continue;
      model.object.position.set(0, 0, 0);
      model.object.updateMatrixWorld(true);
    }
    computeSceneBounds();
    fitAll();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[placement] alignOriginToOrigin", err);
  }
}

export function recenterSceneNow() {
  try {
    const ids = Array.from(ctx.fragments.list.keys());
    if (!ids.length) return;
    const first = ctx.fragments.list.get(ids[0])!;
    const curPos = first.object.position.clone();
    const box = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(first.object);
    if (box.isEmpty()) return;
    sceneOffset = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()).sub(curPos);
    offsetEstablished = true;
    for (const id of ids) applySceneOffsetTo(id);
    computeSceneBounds();
    fitAll();
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[placement] recenterSceneNow", err);
  }
}

export function resetOffsets() {
  sceneOffset = new THREE.Vector3();
  offsetEstablished = true;
  try {
    for (const id of Array.from(ctx.fragments.list.keys())) applySceneOffsetTo(id);
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  computeSceneBounds();
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Projection
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
let currentProjection: Projection = "perspective";

export function getSceneBox(): THREE.Box3 {
  return lastSceneBox.clone();
}
export function getProjection(): Projection {
  if (!worldReady()) return currentProjection;
  const cam: any = ctx.world.camera;
  const t = cam?.cameraType ?? cam?.projection?.current ?? cam?.projection;
  if (typeof t === "string") {
    if (/plan|ortho/i.test(t)) return "orthographic";
    if (/orbit|persp/i.test(t)) return "perspective";
  }
  return isOrthoCam(ctx.world.camera.three) ? "orthographic" : "perspective";
}

function afterProjectionChanged() {
  currentProjection = getProjection();
  try {
    for (const [, model] of ctx.fragments.list) model.useCamera(ctx.world.camera.three);
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  try {
    ctx.fragments.core.update(true);
  } catch {
    /* */
  }

  if (currentProjection === "orthographic") {
    computeSceneBounds();
    const cam = ctx.world.camera.three as THREE.OrthographicCamera;
    if (isOrthoCam(cam)) fitOrthoFrustum(cam, sceneRadius);
  }
  updateClipping();
}

export function setProjection(mode: Projection): boolean {
  if (!worldReady()) {
    currentProjection = mode;
    return false;
  }
  const cam: any = ctx.world.camera;
  if (typeof cam.setCameraType === "function") {
    const attempts =
      mode === "perspective"
        ? ["Orbit", "OrbitCamera", "Perspective"]
        : ["Plan", "PlanCamera", "Orthographic"];
    for (const t of attempts) {
      try {
        cam.setCameraType(t as any);
        afterProjectionChanged();
        return true;
      } catch {
        /* */
      }
    }
  }
  if (cam.projection?.set) {
    try {
      cam.projection.set(mode === "perspective" ? "Perspective" : "Orthographic");
      afterProjectionChanged();
      return true;
    } catch {
      /* */
    }
  }
  console.warn(
    "[projection] could not switch â€” unsupported camera API in this SDK version",
  );
  return false;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Navigation
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
function getCamTarget(out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
  if (!worldReady()) return out.copy(sceneCenter);
  const c: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;
  try {
    if (c.target?.isVector3) return out.copy(c.target);
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  return out.copy(sceneCenter);
}

/**
 * Zoom by factor toward an optional world point (cursor focus).
 * factor > 1 = zoom out, factor < 1 = zoom in.
 */
/**
 * Pure zoom only â€” no orbit, no pan, no target shift (no vibration).
 * factor < 1 = zoom in, factor > 1 = zoom out.
 * Camera moves only along the current look axis; orientation stays fixed.
 */
export function zoomBy(factor: number, focusPoint?: THREE.Vector3) {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  if (!Number.isFinite(factor) || factor <= 0) return;
  factor = Math.min(4.0, Math.max(0.15, factor));

  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three as any;
  const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;
  const target = getCamTarget(new THREE.Vector3());

  try { if (controls && controls.enabled === false) controls.enabled = true; } catch { /* */ }

  // â”€â”€ ORTHOGRAPHIC â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  if (isOrthoCam(cam)) {
    let ortho = cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera;
    if (!(ortho as any).isOrthographicCamera) {
      const _c: any = ctx.world.camera;
      const _cands = [_c.orthographicCamera, _c.orthoCamera];
      for (const c of _cands) {
        const t: any = c?.three ?? c;
        if (t?.isOrthographicCamera) { ortho = t; break; }
      }
    }
    const oldZoom = Math.max(1e-3, ortho.zoom || 1);
    const newZoom = Math.max(0.01, Math.min(5000, oldZoom / factor));
    ortho.zoom = newZoom;
    ortho.updateProjectionMatrix();

    updateClipping();
    return;
  }

  // â”€â”€ PERSPECTIVE â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const offset = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(cam.position, target);
  const dist = offset.length();
  if (dist < 1e-6) return;
  const dir = offset.clone().multiplyScalar(1 / dist);
  const newDist = Math.max(0.05, dist * factor);
  let newPos = target.clone().add(dir.clone().multiplyScalar(newDist));
  let newTarget = target.clone();

  if (focusPoint && Number.isFinite(focusPoint.x)) {
    const viewN = dir.clone();
    const toFocus = focusPoint.clone().sub(target);
    const along = toFocus.dot(viewN);
    const focusOnPlane = focusPoint.clone().sub(viewN.clone().multiplyScalar(along));
    const lateral = focusOnPlane.clone().sub(target);
    const truck = lateral.multiplyScalar(1 - factor);
    newPos.add(truck);
    newTarget.add(truck);
  }

  try {
    controls.setLookAt(
      newPos.x, newPos.y, newPos.z,
      newTarget.x, newTarget.y, newTarget.z,
      false,
    );
  } catch {
    cam.position.copy(newPos);
    try { cam.lookAt(newTarget); } catch { /* */ }
  }
  try { controls.update?.(0); } catch { /* */ }
  updateClipping();
}

export function pickWorldPointUnderCursor(clientX: number, clientY: number): THREE.Vector3 | null {
  try {
    const host = ctx.rightHost;
    const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.width < 1 || rect.height < 1) return null;
    const ndcX = ((clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1;
    const ndcY = -((clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1;
    const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
    const raycaster = new THREE.Raycaster();
    raycaster.setFromCamera(new THREE.Vector2(ndcX, ndcY), cam);

    // Stable plane through the current orbit target, facing the camera.
    // This is the primary anchor so zoom never drifts when the cursor is
    // over empty space or distant background.
    const target = getCamTarget(new THREE.Vector3());
    const normal = new THREE.Vector3();
    cam.getWorldDirection(normal);
    const plane = new THREE.Plane().setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(normal.clone().negate(), target);
    const planeHit = new THREE.Vector3();
    const hasPlane = raycaster.ray.intersectPlane(plane, planeHit);

    // Prefer a nearby mesh hit if it is in front of the camera and not far
    // behind the target plane (keeps zoom "on the model" when hovering it).
    try {
      const hits = raycaster.intersectObjects(ctx.world.scene.three.children, true);
      const camPos = cam.position;
      const targetDist = camPos.distanceTo(target);
      for (const h of hits) {
        if (!h.point || !Number.isFinite(h.point.x)) continue;
        const d = camPos.distanceTo(h.point);
        if (d < 0.01) continue;
        // Accept hits roughly in front of / near the target depth.
        if (d < targetDist * 3 + 50) return h.point.clone();
      }
    } catch { /* */ }

    if (hasPlane && Number.isFinite(planeHit.x)) return planeHit;
  } catch { /* */ }
  return null;
}

export type ExactView = "top" | "bottom" | "front" | "back" | "right" | "left";

/**
 * Pure Revit-style cardinal view. Unlike gotoDir(), this deliberately ignores
 * Project North and the current camera rotation so Top really means +Y,
 * Front +Z, Right +X, etc. All cube-face and explicit view commands should use
 * this function to guarantee identical camera behavior.
 */
export function gotoExactView(view: ExactView): void {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  const target = getCamTarget(new THREE.Vector3());
  const table: Record<ExactView, { dir: THREE.Vector3; up: THREE.Vector3 }> = {
    top: { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1) },
    bottom: { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, -1, 0), up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1) },
    front: { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0) },
    back: { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1), up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0) },
    right: { dir: new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0) },
    left: { dir: new THREE.Vector3(-1, 0, 0), up: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0) },
  };
  const spec = table[view];
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;

  // In ortho, distance does not affect visible size; keep the camera close
  // so near/far clipping stays small and precise.
  const isOrtho = isOrthoCam(cam);
  const dist = isOrtho
    ? Math.max(sceneRadius * 1.5, 5)
    : Math.max(sceneRadius * 2, 10);
  const pos = target.clone().add(spec.dir.clone().multiplyScalar(dist));

  cam.up.copy(spec.up);
  ctx.world.camera.controls.setLookAt(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, target.x, target.y, target.z, true);
  cam.up.copy(spec.up);
  cam.lookAt(target);

  if (isOrtho) {
    fitOrthoFrustum(cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera, sceneRadius);
  }
  updateClipping();
}

export function gotoDir(dir: THREE.Vector3) {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  const target = getCamTarget(new THREE.Vector3());
  const dist = Math.max(
    ctx.world.camera.three.position.distanceTo(target),
    sceneRadius,
    0.5,
  );
  const pos = target.clone().add(dir.clone().normalize().multiplyScalar(dist));
  if (Math.abs(dir.y) > 0.99) {
    pos.x += dist * 0.002;
    pos.z += dist * 0.002;
  }
  ctx.world.camera.controls.setLookAt(
    pos.x,
    pos.y,
    pos.z,
    target.x,
    target.y,
    target.z,
    true,
  );
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  if (isOrthoCam(cam)) fitOrthoFrustum(cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera, sceneRadius);
  updateClipping();
}

export function focusOnPoint(point: THREE.Vector3) {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  // Remember current camera so Focus does not jump back to the start/home view
  preFocusView = saveCameraSnapshot();
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;

  const camToTarget = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(cam.position, point);
  const horizontalDist = Math.hypot(camToTarget.x, camToTarget.z);
  let dir: THREE.Vector3;
  if (horizontalDist < 0.05) {
    dir = new THREE.Vector3(1.0, 0.7, 1.0).normalize();
  } else {
    const azimuth = Math.atan2(camToTarget.x, camToTarget.z);
    const pitchRaw = Math.atan2(camToTarget.y, horizontalDist);
    const pitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(
      pitchRaw,
      THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(-30),
      THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(60),
    );
    const cosP = Math.cos(pitch);
    dir = new THREE.Vector3(
      Math.sin(azimuth) * cosP,
      Math.sin(pitch),
      Math.cos(azimuth) * cosP,
    ).normalize();
  }

  // Fit so the object fills ~60% of the view (Revit Focus)
  computeSceneBounds();
  const targetDist = Math.max(sceneRadius * 0.12, 1.5, 0.5);
  const pos = point.clone().add(dir.multiplyScalar(targetDist * 1.8));
  try {
    controls.setLookAt(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, point.x, point.y, point.z, true);
  } catch {
    cam.position.copy(pos);
    cam.lookAt(point);
  }
  if (isOrthoCam(cam)) {
    fitOrthoFrustum(cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera, Math.max(targetDist * 0.55, 0.8));
    cam.zoom = Math.max(cam.zoom || 1, 1.4);
    cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
  }
  updateClipping();
}

/** Focus a world-space box while preserving the current view direction. */

/**
 * Pin the orbit pivot to a world point without changing camera position.
 * Shift+MMB orbit will rotate around this point until the target is changed again.
 */
export function setOrbitPivot(point: THREE.Vector3): void {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  if (!point || !Number.isFinite(point.x)) return;
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;
  if (!controls) return;
  const pos = cam.position.clone();

  // Direct write to the internal target first (works on all versions).
  try {
    if (controls.target?.isVector3) {
      controls.target.set(point.x, point.y, point.z);
    }
  } catch { /* */ }

  // Also tell camera-controls â€” this updates its interpolation endpoints.
  try {
    if (typeof controls.setTarget === "function") {
      controls.setTarget(point.x, point.y, point.z, false);
    }
  } catch { /* */ }

  // Force a look-at so the camera orientation matches the new target
  // even in versions where setTarget only updates the end state.
  try {
    if (typeof controls.setLookAt === "function") {
      controls.setLookAt(pos.x, pos.y, pos.z, point.x, point.y, point.z, false);
    } else {
      cam.lookAt(point);
    }
  } catch { /* */ }

  try { controls.update?.(0); } catch { /* */ }
  try { updateClipping(); } catch { /* */ }
}

/**
 * Pan the camera so that `point` sits at the screen center, WITHOUT
 * changing view direction or zoom. Sets the orbit target to `point`.
 */
export function centerOnPoint(point: THREE.Vector3): void {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  if (!point || !Number.isFinite(point.x)) return;
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;
  if (!controls) return;

  // Read current target
  const oldTarget = new THREE.Vector3();
  if (controls.target?.isVector3) oldTarget.copy(controls.target);
  else {
    const dir = new THREE.Vector3();
    cam.getWorldDirection(dir);
    oldTarget.copy(cam.position).add(dir.multiplyScalar(5));
  }

  // Pure translation: camera moves by delta, target becomes point.
  const delta = point.clone().sub(oldTarget);
  const newPos = cam.position.clone().add(delta);

  try {
    if (typeof controls.setLookAt === "function") {
      controls.setLookAt(newPos.x, newPos.y, newPos.z, point.x, point.y, point.z, true);
    } else {
      cam.position.copy(newPos);
      cam.lookAt(point);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[camera] centerOnPoint failed", err);
  }

  // After the smooth transition finishes, re-assert the pivot so
  // subsequent Shift+MMB orbits stay centered.
  setTimeout(() => {
    try { setOrbitPivot(point); } catch { /* */ }
  }, 220);

  try { updateClipping(); } catch { /* */ }
}

/** Resolve a sensible pivot from current fragment + authored selection. */
export async function pivotOnSelection(
  idMap?: Record<string, Set<number>> | null,
): Promise<boolean> {
  if (!worldReady()) return false;
  const box = new THREE.Box3();
  let found = false;

  // â”€â”€ Fragment selection (use WORLD boxes, not local) â”€â”€
  try {
    const map = idMap && Object.keys(idMap).length
      ? idMap
      : (await import("./selection")).selection.snapshot();
    for (const [modelId, ids] of Object.entries(map ?? {})) {
      const model: any = ctx.fragments.list.get(modelId);
      if (!model || !ids || !(ids as Set<number>).size) continue;
      try {
        const wbs = await getElementWorldBoxes(model, [...(ids as Set<number>)], modelId);
        for (const wb of wbs) {
          if (wb?.box && !wb.box.isEmpty()) { box.union(wb.box); found = true; }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[camera] pivot world-box read failed", modelId, err);
      }
    }
  } catch { /* */ }

  // â”€â”€ Authored selection â”€â”€
  try {
    const auth: any = (ctx.features as any).authoring;
    const ids: string[] = auth?.getSelectedIds?.() ?? [];
    for (const id of ids) {
      const el = auth?.getElementById?.(id);
      if (!el) continue;
      const wb = auth?.getElementWorldBox?.(el);
      if (wb && !wb.isEmpty()) { box.union(wb); found = true; }
    }
  } catch { /* */ }

  if (!found || box.isEmpty()) return false;
  const center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  setOrbitPivot(center);
  return true;
}

export function focusOnBox(box: THREE.Box3): void {
  if (!worldReady() || box.isEmpty()) return;
  preFocusView = saveCameraSnapshot();

  const center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const radius = Math.max(box.getBoundingSphere(new THREE.Sphere()).radius, 0.5);
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;

  const camToCenter = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(cam.position, center);
  const horizontalDist = Math.hypot(camToCenter.x, camToCenter.z);
  let dir: THREE.Vector3;
  if (horizontalDist < Math.max(radius * 0.15, 0.05)) {
    dir = new THREE.Vector3(1, 0.7, 1).normalize();
  } else {
    const azimuth = Math.atan2(camToCenter.x, camToCenter.z);
    const pitchRaw = Math.atan2(camToCenter.y, horizontalDist);
    const pitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(
      pitchRaw,
      THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(-30),
      THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(60),
    );
    const cosP = Math.cos(pitch);
    dir = new THREE.Vector3(
      Math.sin(azimuth) * cosP,
      Math.sin(pitch),
      Math.cos(azimuth) * cosP,
    ).normalize();
  }

  if (isOrthoCam(cam)) {
    const dist = Math.max(radius * 1.5, 5);
    controls.setLookAt(
      center.x + dir.x * dist,
      center.y + dir.y * dist,
      center.z + dir.z * dist,
      center.x, center.y, center.z,
      true,
    );
    cam.up.set(0, 1, 0);
    cam.lookAt(center);
    fitOrthoFrustum(cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera, radius);
  } else {
    const persp = cam as THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
    const vFov = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(persp.fov ?? 60);
    const dist = (radius / Math.sin(Math.max(vFov, 0.3) / 2)) * 1.15;
    controls.setLookAt(
      center.x + dir.x * dist,
      center.y + dir.y * dist,
      center.z + dir.z * dist,
      center.x, center.y, center.z,
      true,
    );
  }
  updateClipping();
}

/**
 * Handles a stationary RMB on geometry. Returns true when an object was hit,
 * so the context menu can remain closed. A dragged RMB is handled separately
 * by the camera navigation / Ctrl-crossing selection gesture.
 */
export async function handleRightClickFocus(e: MouseEvent): Promise<boolean> {
  // Auto right-click focus disabled: explicit context-menu Focus preserves selection.
  void e;
  return false;
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Home view
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
let homeView: { pos: THREE.Vector3; target: THREE.Vector3; zoom?: number } | null = null;
let preFocusView: { pos: THREE.Vector3; target: THREE.Vector3; zoom?: number } | null = null;


export function saveCameraSnapshot(): { pos: THREE.Vector3; target: THREE.Vector3; zoom?: number } | null {
  if (!worldReady()) return null;
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three as any;
  return {
    pos: cam.position.clone(),
    target: getCamTarget(new THREE.Vector3()).clone(),
    zoom: isOrthoCam(cam) ? Number(cam.zoom) : undefined,
  };
}
export function restoreCameraSnapshot(snap: { pos: THREE.Vector3; target: THREE.Vector3; zoom?: number } | null): void {
  if (!snap || !worldReady()) return;
  const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three as any;
  try {
    controls.setLookAt(snap.pos.x, snap.pos.y, snap.pos.z, snap.target.x, snap.target.y, snap.target.z, true);
  } catch {
    cam.position.copy(snap.pos);
  }
  if (snap.zoom != null && isOrthoCam(cam)) {
    cam.zoom = snap.zoom;
    cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
  }
  updateClipping();
}

export function saveHome() {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  homeView = {
    pos: ctx.world.camera.three.position.clone(),
    target: getCamTarget(new THREE.Vector3()).clone(),
  };
}

export function goHome() {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  if (homeView) {
    ctx.world.camera.controls.setLookAt(
      homeView.pos.x,
      homeView.pos.y,
      homeView.pos.z,
      homeView.target.x,
      homeView.target.y,
      homeView.target.z,
      true,
    );
    const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
    if (isOrthoCam(cam)) fitOrthoFrustum(cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera, sceneRadius);
    updateClipping();
  } else fitAll();
}

export function fitAll() {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  computeSceneBounds();

  if (lastSceneBox.isEmpty()) {
    const modelCount = (() => { try { return ctx.fragments.list.size; } catch { return 0; } })();
    if (modelCount > 0) {
      console.warn(`[camera] fitAll: ${modelCount} model(s) present but scene bounds are empty â€” retrying in 200 ms.`);
      setTimeout(() => { try { fitAll(); } catch { /* */ } }, 200);
    }
    return;
  }

  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;
  const dir = new THREE.Vector3(1.2, 0.7, 1.2).normalize();

  if (isOrthoCam(cam)) {
    // Orthographic: the frustum IS the fit. Position the camera a
    // comfortable distance from the scene center along the look dir.
    let ortho = cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera;
    if (!(ortho as any).isOrthographicCamera) {
      const _c: any = ctx.world.camera;
      const _cands = [_c.orthographicCamera, _c.orthoCamera];
      for (const c of _cands) {
        const t: any = c?.three ?? c;
        if (t?.isOrthographicCamera) { ortho = t; break; }
      }
    }
    const dist = Math.max(sceneRadius * 1.5, 5);
    controls.setLookAt(
      sceneCenter.x + dir.x * dist,
      sceneCenter.y + dir.y * dist,
      sceneCenter.z + dir.z * dist,
      sceneCenter.x, sceneCenter.y, sceneCenter.z,
      false,
    );
    cam.up.set(0, 1, 0);
    cam.lookAt(sceneCenter);
    fitOrthoFrustum(ortho, sceneRadius);
  } else {
    const persp = cam as THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
    const vFov = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(persp.fov ?? 60);
    const dist = (sceneRadius / Math.sin(Math.max(vFov, 0.3) / 2)) * 1.2;
    controls.setLookAt(
      sceneCenter.x + dir.x * dist,
      sceneCenter.y + dir.y * dist,
      sceneCenter.z + dir.z * dist,
      sceneCenter.x, sceneCenter.y, sceneCenter.z,
      false,
    );
  }

  updateClipping();
  refreshBoundsInfo();
  if (!homeView) saveHome();
}

/**
 * Zoom the camera to fit a specific set of elements.
 *
 * Uses the SDK-corrected world boxes from `world-box.ts` so the camera
 * lands on the geometry as rendered â€” not on the pre-alignment location.
 *
 * `modelId` is passed through to `getElementWorldBoxes` so that a stale
 * entry (worker no longer has the model) can be pruned by safe-query
 * instead of throwing "Fragments: Model not found".
 */
export async function fitToMap(map: OBC.ModelIdMap) {
  if (!worldReady()) return;
  preFocusView = saveCameraSnapshot();

  const box = new THREE.Box3();

  for (const [modelId, ids] of Object.entries(map)) {
    const model: any = ctx.fragments.list.get(modelId);
    if (!model || !ids?.size) continue;
    try {
      const boxes = await getElementWorldBoxes(model, Array.from(ids), modelId);
      for (const wb of boxes) {
        if (!wb.box.isEmpty()) box.union(wb.box);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("[camera] fitToMap box read failed", modelId, err);
    }
  }

  if (box.isEmpty()) {
    fitAll();
    return;
  }

  const center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
  const radius = Math.max(box.getBoundingSphere(new THREE.Sphere()).radius, 0.5);

  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;

  // Natural viewing direction: keep azimuth, clamp pitch.
  const camToCenter = new THREE.Vector3().subVectors(cam.position, center);
  const horizontalDist = Math.hypot(camToCenter.x, camToCenter.z);
  let dir: THREE.Vector3;

  if (horizontalDist < Math.max(radius * 0.15, 0.05)) {
    dir = new THREE.Vector3(1.0, 0.7, 1.0).normalize();
  } else {
    const azimuth = Math.atan2(camToCenter.x, camToCenter.z);
    const pitchRaw = Math.atan2(camToCenter.y, horizontalDist);
    const pitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(
      pitchRaw,
      THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(-30),
      THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(60),
    );
    const cosP = Math.cos(pitch);
    dir = new THREE.Vector3(
      Math.sin(azimuth) * cosP,
      Math.sin(pitch),
      Math.cos(azimuth) * cosP,
    ).normalize();
  }

  if (isOrthoCam(cam)) {
    fitOrthoFrustum(cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera, radius);
    const dist = radius * 4;
    ctx.world.camera.controls.setLookAt(
      center.x + dir.x * dist,
      center.y + dir.y * dist,
      center.z + dir.z * dist,
      center.x,
      center.y,
      center.z,
      true,
    );
  } else {
    const persp = cam as THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
    const vFov = THREE.MathUtils.degToRad(persp.fov ?? 60);
    const dist = (radius / Math.sin(Math.max(vFov, 0.3) / 2)) * 1.15;
    ctx.world.camera.controls.setLookAt(
      center.x + dir.x * dist,
      center.y + dir.y * dist,
      center.z + dir.z * dist,
      center.x,
      center.y,
      center.z,
      true,
    );
  }

  updateClipping();
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Recovery
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
export function recoverView(): void {
  try {
    const modelCount = ctx.fragments.list.size;
    computeSceneBounds();
    const boxSize = new THREE.Vector3();
    lastSceneBox.getSize(boxSize);

    const cam: any = ctx.world.camera.three;
    const isOrtho = isOrthoCam(cam);
    console.log(
      `[camera] recoverView Â· models=${modelCount} Â· ` +
        `scene ${boxSize.x.toFixed(1)}Ã—${boxSize.y.toFixed(1)}Ã—${boxSize.z.toFixed(1)} m Â· ` +
        `center (${sceneCenter.x.toFixed(1)}, ${sceneCenter.y.toFixed(1)}, ${sceneCenter.z.toFixed(1)}) Â· ` +
        `radius ${sceneRadius.toFixed(1)} Â· ` +
        `camera ${isOrtho ? "ortho" : "persp"} Â· ` +
        `position (${cam.position.x.toFixed(1)}, ${cam.position.y.toFixed(1)}, ${cam.position.z.toFixed(1)}) Â· ` +
        `near ${cam.near} Â· far ${cam.far}`,
    );

    if (lastSceneBox.isEmpty()) {
      console.warn("[camera] recoverView: scene bounds are empty.");
      return;
    }

    fitAll();
    try {
      ctx.fragments.core.update(true);
    } catch {
      /* */
    }

    const proj = new THREE.Matrix4().multiplyMatrices(
      cam.projectionMatrix,
      cam.matrixWorldInverse,
    );
    const ndc = sceneCenter.clone().applyMatrix4(proj);
    if (Math.abs(ndc.x) > 2 || Math.abs(ndc.y) > 2 || ndc.z < -2 || ndc.z > 2) {
      console.warn(
        `[camera] recoverView: scene center still outside frustum ` +
          `(ndc ${ndc.x.toFixed(2)}, ${ndc.y.toFixed(2)}, ${ndc.z.toFixed(2)}). ` +
          `Switching to a fresh isometric view.`,
      );

      const dist = Math.max(sceneRadius * 3, 5);
      const dir = new THREE.Vector3(1.2, 0.7, 1.2).normalize();
      ctx.world.camera.controls.setLookAt(
        sceneCenter.x + dir.x * dist,
        sceneCenter.y + dir.y * dist,
        sceneCenter.z + dir.z * dist,
        sceneCenter.x,
        sceneCenter.y,
        sceneCenter.z,
        false,
      );
      if (isOrtho) {
        fitOrthoFrustum(cam as THREE.OrthographicCamera, sceneRadius);
      }
      updateClipping();
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[camera] recoverView failed", err);
  }
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Screenshot
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
export interface ScreenshotOptions {
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  format?: "png" | "jpeg";
  quality?: number;
  filename?: string;
}

export type ScreenshotPreset = "current" | "hd" | "4k" | "8k";

const SCREENSHOT_PRESETS: Record<ScreenshotPreset, { w: number; h: number } | null> = {
  current: null,
  hd: { w: 1920, h: 1080 },
  "4k": { w: 3840, h: 2160 },
  "8k": { w: 7680, h: 4320 },
};

const SCREENSHOT_KEY = "obs.screenshot.preset";

let currentPreset: ScreenshotPreset = "hd";
try {
  const raw = localStorage.getItem(SCREENSHOT_KEY);
  if (raw === "current" || raw === "hd" || raw === "4k" || raw === "8k") {
    currentPreset = raw;
  }
} catch {
  /* */
}

export function getScreenshotPreset(): ScreenshotPreset {
  return currentPreset;
}

export function setScreenshotPreset(p: ScreenshotPreset): void {
  currentPreset = p;
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(SCREENSHOT_KEY, p);
  } catch {
    /* */
  }
  const size = SCREENSHOT_PRESETS[p];
  console.log(`[screenshot] preset â†’ ${p}${size ? ` (${size.w}Ã—${size.h})` : ""}`);
}

export function takeScreenshot(opts: ScreenshotOptions = {}): void {
  console.log(`[screenshot] click Â· preset="${currentPreset}"`);
  if (!worldReady()) {
    console.warn("[screenshot] world not ready â€” nothing to capture");
    return;
  }
  const renderer = ctx.world.renderer.three as THREE.WebGLRenderer;
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const scene = ctx.world.scene.three;
  if (!renderer || !cam || !scene) {
    console.warn("[screenshot] renderer/camera/scene missing");
    return;
  }

  const preset = SCREENSHOT_PRESETS[currentPreset];
  const targetW = Math.max(
    64,
    Math.round(opts.width ?? preset?.w ?? renderer.domElement.width),
  );
  const targetH = Math.max(
    64,
    Math.round(opts.height ?? preset?.h ?? renderer.domElement.height),
  );

  console.log(`[screenshot] rendering ${targetW}Ã—${targetH}`);

  // â”€â”€ Save camera state â”€â”€
  const prevPerspAspect = (cam as THREE.PerspectiveCamera).aspect;
  const prevOrtho =
    cam instanceof THREE.OrthographicCamera
      ? {
          left: cam.left,
          right: cam.right,
          top: cam.top,
          bottom: cam.bottom,
          zoom: cam.zoom,
        }
      : null;

  // â”€â”€ Save renderer state â”€â”€
  const prevTarget = renderer.getRenderTarget();
  const prevAutoClear = renderer.autoClear;
  const prevClearColor = new THREE.Color();
  const prevClearAlpha = renderer.getClearAlpha();
  renderer.getClearColor(prevClearColor);

  const format = opts.format ?? "png";
  const mime = format === "jpeg" ? "image/jpeg" : "image/png";
  const quality = opts.quality ?? 0.95;

  let out: HTMLCanvasElement | null = null;
  let rt: THREE.WebGLRenderTarget | null = null;

  try {
    // â”€â”€ Adjust camera aspect for the target resolution â”€â”€
    if (cam instanceof THREE.PerspectiveCamera) {
      cam.aspect = targetW / targetH;
      cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
    } else if (cam instanceof THREE.OrthographicCamera) {
      const h = cam.top - cam.bottom;
      const w = h * (targetW / targetH);
      const cx = (cam.left + cam.right) / 2;
      cam.left = cx - w / 2;
      cam.right = cx + w / 2;
      cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
    }

    // â”€â”€ Offscreen render target â”€â”€
    // The main WebGL context was created without preserveDrawingBuffer,
    // so `renderer.domElement.toDataURL()` returns a black canvas on
    // Chrome / Edge. A render target is a texture we own; its contents
    // survive the render call and can be read back with readRenderTargetPixels.
    console.log("[screenshot] step 1/5 Â· creating render target");
    rt = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(targetW, targetH, {
      format: THREE.RGBAFormat,
      type: THREE.UnsignedByteType,
      depthBuffer: true,
      stencilBuffer: false,
      minFilter: THREE.LinearFilter,
      magFilter: THREE.LinearFilter,
      generateMipmaps: false,
    });
    // Match the renderer's output color space so the pixels we read back
    // look the same as what's on screen. Without this, Three.js writes
    // linear values into the RT and the image looks washed out / dark.
    try {
      const outputCS = (renderer as any).outputColorSpace;
      if (outputCS) (rt.texture as any).colorSpace = outputCS;
    } catch {
      /* */
    }

    console.log("[screenshot] step 2/5 Â· rendering scene â†’ RT");
    renderer.autoClear = true;
    renderer.setRenderTarget(rt);
    renderer.clear(true, true, true);
    renderer.render(scene, cam);

    console.log("[screenshot] step 3/5 Â· reading pixels back");
    const pixels = new Uint8Array(targetW * targetH * 4);
    renderer.readRenderTargetPixels(rt, 0, 0, targetW, targetH, pixels);

    // Restore renderer immediately.
    renderer.setRenderTarget(prevTarget);
    renderer.autoClear = prevAutoClear;
    renderer.setClearColor(prevClearColor, prevClearAlpha);
    rt.dispose();
    rt = null;

    // â”€â”€ Sparse sanity scan â”€â”€
    const cx = targetW >> 1;
    const cy = targetH >> 1;
    const sample = (cy * targetW + cx) * 4;
    const r0 = pixels[sample];
    const g0 = pixels[sample + 1];
    const b0 = pixels[sample + 2];
    let nonBlack = 0;
    const stepX = Math.max(1, targetW >> 6);
    const stepY = Math.max(1, targetH >> 6);
    for (let y = 0; y < targetH; y += stepY) {
      for (let x = 0; x < targetW; x += stepX) {
        const i = (y * targetW + x) * 4;
        if (pixels[i] > 8 || pixels[i + 1] > 8 || pixels[i + 2] > 8) nonBlack++;
      }
    }
    if (nonBlack < 4) {
      console.warn(
        `[screenshot] render target appears entirely black (center ${r0},${g0},${b0}). ` +
          "Likely: no models loaded, or camera inside geometry.",
      );
    } else {
      console.log(`[screenshot] pixel sanity OK Â· ${nonBlack} non-black sample(s)`);
    }

    // â”€â”€ Flip Y into a 2D canvas â”€â”€
    // readRenderTargetPixels returns rows bottom-up; a 2D canvas is top-down.
    console.log("[screenshot] step 4/5 Â· composing output canvas");
    out = document.createElement("canvas");
    out.width = targetW;
    out.height = targetH;
    const outCtx = out.getContext("2d");
    if (!outCtx) throw new Error("Could not acquire 2D context for output canvas.");

    const imgData = outCtx.createImageData(targetW, targetH);
    const rowBytes = targetW * 4;
    for (let y = 0; y < targetH; y++) {
      const src = (targetH - 1 - y) * rowBytes;
      imgData.data.set(pixels.subarray(src, src + rowBytes), y * rowBytes);
    }
    outCtx.putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
  } catch (err) {
    if (rt) {
      try {
        renderer.setRenderTarget(prevTarget);
        renderer.autoClear = prevAutoClear;
        renderer.setClearColor(prevClearColor, prevClearAlpha);
        rt.dispose();
      } catch {
        /* */
      }
    }
    console.error("[screenshot] capture failed", err);
    alert(
      "Screenshot failed.\n\n" +
        (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)) +
        "\n\nTry a smaller preset (HD or Native).",
    );
    return;
  } finally {
    // â”€â”€ Restore camera â”€â”€
    try {
      if (
        cam instanceof THREE.PerspectiveCamera &&
        typeof prevPerspAspect === "number"
      ) {
        cam.aspect = prevPerspAspect;
        cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
      } else if (prevOrtho && cam instanceof THREE.OrthographicCamera) {
        cam.left = prevOrtho.left;
        cam.right = prevOrtho.right;
        cam.top = prevOrtho.top;
        cam.bottom = prevOrtho.bottom;
        cam.zoom = prevOrtho.zoom;
        cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
      }
      try {
        ctx.fragments.core.update(true);
      } catch {
        /* */
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("[screenshot] restore failed", err);
    }
  }

  if (!out) return;

  // â”€â”€ Encode + download â”€â”€
  // We use toBlob rather than toDataURL because 4K and 8K PNGs exceed
  // Chrome's ~2 MB data-URL cap, which silently truncates the download.
  const date = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 19).replace(/[:T]/g, "-");
  const filename =
    opts.filename ??
    `open-bim-studio-${targetW}x${targetH}-${date}.${opts.format ?? "png"}`;

  console.log("[screenshot] step 5/5 Â· encoding blobâ€¦");
  try {
    out.toBlob(
      (blob) => {
        if (!blob) {
          console.error("[screenshot] toBlob returned null â€” try a smaller preset");
          alert("Encoding failed. Try a smaller preset (HD or Native).");
          return;
        }
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.href = url;
        a.download = filename;
        a.style.display = "none";
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        setTimeout(() => {
          a.remove();
          URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        }, 5_000);
        console.log(
          `[screenshot] saved ${filename} Â· ${targetW}Ã—${targetH} Â· ` +
            `${(blob.size / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB`,
        );
      },
      mime,
      quality,
    );
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("[screenshot] toBlob threw", err);
    alert(
      "Screenshot encoding failed.\n\n" +
        (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)),
    );
  }
}

/* â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
   Setup
   â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â• */
export function setupCameraTools() {
  ctx.world.scene.three.add(boundsHelperGroup);
  ctx.world.camera.controls.addEventListener("update", () => updateClipping());
  currentProjection = getProjection();
  applyClipping(0.1, 20000);

  // MMB / wheel / dbl-MMB owned by features/navigation.ts via input-router.
  try {
    const controls: any = ctx.world.camera.controls;
    if (controls) {
      if ("dollyToCursor" in controls) controls.dollyToCursor = false;
      if ("infinityDolly" in controls) controls.infinityDolly = true;
      if ("smoothTime" in controls) controls.smoothTime = 0.04;
      if ("draggingSmoothTime" in controls) controls.draggingSmoothTime = 0.02;
      if ("azimuthRotateSpeed" in controls) controls.azimuthRotateSpeed = 1.0;
      if ("polarRotateSpeed" in controls) controls.polarRotateSpeed = 1.0;
      if ("truckSpeed" in controls) controls.truckSpeed = 1.0;
      
      try { if ("dollySpeed" in controls) controls.dollySpeed = 0; } catch { /* */ }


      const ACTION = controls.constructor?.ACTION || (window as any).CameraControls?.ACTION;
      if (ACTION && controls.mouseButtons) {
        controls.mouseButtons.left = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        controls.mouseButtons.middle = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        controls.mouseButtons.right = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        controls.mouseButtons.wheel = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[camera] mouse defaults", e);
  }

  // Re-assert after a tick â€” some OBC versions re-enable defaults asynchronously.
  setTimeout(() => {
    try {
      const controls: any = ctx.world?.camera?.controls;
      const ACTION = controls?.constructor?.ACTION || (window as any).CameraControls?.ACTION;
      if (ACTION && controls?.mouseButtons) {
        controls.mouseButtons.left = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        controls.mouseButtons.middle = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        controls.mouseButtons.right = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
        controls.mouseButtons.wheel = ACTION.NONE ?? 0;
      }
      forceResumeCameraControls();
    } catch { /* */ }
  }, 0);

  (window as any).__obsRmbDragging = false;

  // Clipping is driven by the camera-controls "update" listener above.
  // Avoid a permanent rAF loop â€” it fights navigation and burns CPU.

  Object.assign(ctx.scene, {
    center: sceneCenter,
    box: lastSceneBox,
    computeBounds: computeSceneBounds,
    establishOffset: establishSceneOffset,
    recenter: recenterSceneNow,
    alignOriginToOrigin,
    resetOffsets,
    fitAll,
    fitToMap,
  });

  Object.assign(ctx.cam, {
    updateClipping,
    setProjection,
    zoomBy,
    gotoDir,
    saveHome,
    goHome,
    screenshot: takeScreenshot,
    screenshotPreset: getScreenshotPreset,
    setScreenshotPreset,
    recoverView,
  
    viewExact: (view: ExactView) => gotoExactView(view),
    fitAll,
});
  (ctx.features as any).viewExact = (view: ExactView) => gotoExactView(view);

  const cam: CameraService = {
    fitAll,
    fitToMap,
    focusOnPoint,
    focusOnBox,
    zoomBy,
    gotoDir,
    gotoExactView,
    setProjection,
    getProjection,
    saveHome,
    goHome,
    screenshot: takeScreenshot,
    getScreenshotPreset,
    setScreenshotPreset,
    computeSceneBounds,
    recoverView,
    updateClipping,
    scene: {
      center: sceneCenter,
      box: lastSceneBox,
      radius: () => sceneRadius,
    },
  };
  register("camera", cam);
}

===== FILE: src/features/viewcube.ts =====
/**
 * ViewCube Pro â€” Revit-style orientation widget.
 *
 * Pick zones (26): 6 faces Â· 12 edges Â· 8 corners.
 * Right-click menu + bottom button row (Home Â· Fit Â· Roll Â±90Â°).
 * Native projection only â€” no ortho/perspective toggle.
 * World north only â€” Project North / True North UI removed.
 *
 * All state-dependent initialization happens inside setupViewCube().
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";
import {
  gotoDir, gotoExactView, fitAll, goHome, setProjection, getProjection,
} from "./camera-tools";

/** Revit: Project North angle (degrees, CCW from True/world +Z as Front). */
let projectNorthDeg = 0;
/** Active orientation mode for the compass & face clicks. */
let northMode: "project" | "true" = "project";

export function getProjectNorthDeg() { return projectNorthDeg; }
export function getNorthMode() { return northMode; }

export function setProjectNorthDeg(deg: number) {
  projectNorthDeg = ((deg % 360) + 360) % 360;
  updateNorthReadout();
}

export function setNorthMode(mode: "project" | "true") {
  northMode = mode;
  updateNorthReadout();
  try {
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("obs-toast", {
      detail: {
        message: mode === "project" ? "Orientation: Project North" : "Orientation: True North",
        kind: "info",
      },
    }));
  } catch { /* */ }
}

/** World-space +Z is FRONT at 0Â° True North. Project North rotates that. */
function northOffsetRad(): number {
  // Project / True North toggles removed â€” always world (true) north.
  return 0;
}

function applyNorthToDir(dir: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
  const off = northOffsetRad();
  if (Math.abs(off) < 1e-8) return dir.clone().normalize();
  // Rotate around world Y (up)
  const q = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), off);
  return dir.clone().applyQuaternion(q).normalize();
}

function gotoDirOriented(dir: THREE.Vector3, _forceOrthoFace = true) {
  // Keep native projection; only orient the camera.
  gotoDir(applyNorthToDir(dir));
}

  try {
    const mode = northMode === "project" ? "PN" : "TN";
    const ang = Math.round(projectNorthDeg);
    readout.textContent = `${mode} ${ang}Â°`;
  } catch { /* */ }
}
