/* â”€â”€ Event listeners on the cube â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */
canvas.addEventListener("pointerdown", (e) => {
  if (e.button === 2) return;
  if (e.button !== 0) return;
  e.stopPropagation();
  cubeDragging = true;
  cubeDown = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
  canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
  canvas.style.cursor = "grabbing";
});

canvas.addEventListener("pointermove", (e) => {
  e.stopPropagation();
  if (cubeDragging && cubeDown) {
    const dx = e.clientX - cubeDown.x;
    const dy = e.clientY - cubeDown.y;
    cubeDown = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
    const c: any = ctx.world.camera?.controls;
    try {
      if (typeof c?.rotate === "function") c.rotate(dx * 0.008, -dy * 0.008);
      else if (typeof c?.rotateLeft === "function") {
        c.rotateLeft(dx * 0.008);
        c.rotateUp(-dy * 0.008);
      }
    } catch { /* */ }
    return;
  }
  const pick = pickZone(e);
  setHover(!!pick);
  readout.textContent = pick ? pick.label : "";
});

canvas.addEventListener("pointerup", (e) => {
  if (e.button !== 0 || !cubeDragging) return;
  e.stopPropagation();
  cubeDragging = false;
  canvas.style.cursor = "grab";
  const moved = cubeDown ? Math.hypot(e.clientX - cubeDown.x, e.clientY - cubeDown.y) : 0;
  cubeDown = null;
  if (moved < 5) onClick(e);
});

canvas.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => {
  if (!cubeDragging) setHover(false);
  readout.textContent = "";
});

canvas.addEventListener("dblclick", (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); fitAll(); });

canvas.addEventListener("contextmenu", (e) => {
  e.preventDefault();
  e.stopPropagation();
  openCubeMenu(e);
});

/* â”€â”€ Setup (ViewCube removed from platform) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */
export function setupViewCube() {
  // Do not mount or animate the cube.
  try {
    cubeHost.style.display = "none";
    cubeHost.remove();
  } catch { /* */ }

  // openbim-fix: viewcube.ts -- release the WebGL renderer + context
  try {
    renderer.dispose();
    const gl: any = (renderer as any).getContext?.();
    if (gl) {
      const ext = gl.getExtension("WEBGL_lose_context");
      ext?.loseContext?.();
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[viewcube] renderer dispose failed", err);
  }

    ctx.features.viewcube = {
    host: cubeHost,
    rollLeft: () => { /* removed */ },
    rollRight: () => { /* removed */ },
    setProjectNorth: setProjectNorthDeg,
    getProjectNorth: getProjectNorthDeg,
    setNorthMode,
    getNorthMode,
  };
  console.log("[viewcube] disabled â€” 3D cube not shown");
}


===== FILE: src/features/section.ts =====
/**
 * Section planes â€” persistence and viewpoint integration.
 *
 * Reads and writes the clipper state defensively (SDK API varies by version).
 * Section planes are saved into issue viewpoints via the BCF exporter
 * (see modules/issues/bcf.ts) and into projects via appState.sections.
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import * as OBC from "@thatopen/components";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";
import { isMeasureEnabled, setMeasureEnabled } from "./measure";
import { isDrawingActive } from "./drawing";

export interface SectionPlaneState {
  location: [number, number, number];
  direction: [number, number, number];
}

let enabled = false;
let checkboxEl: any = null;

export function setSectionCheckboxRef(el: any) {
  checkboxEl = el;
}
export function isSectionOn() {
  return enabled;
}

export function setSectionOn(on: boolean) {
  enabled = on;
  const el = checkboxEl ?? ctx.leftHost.querySelector("bim-checkbox[data-section]");
  if (el) el.checked = on;
}

export function toggleSection() {
  const next = !enabled;
  setSectionOn(next);
  if (next) setMeasureEnabled(false);
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// Read current planes
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
export function getSectionPlanes(): SectionPlaneState[] {
  const out: SectionPlaneState[] = [];
  try {
    const list = (ctx.clipper as any)?.list;
    if (!list) return out;
    const iterable: any = list[Symbol.iterator] ? list : null;
    if (!iterable) return out;
    for (const entry of iterable) {
      const item = Array.isArray(entry) ? entry[1] : entry;
      const plane: THREE.Plane = item?.three ?? item?.plane ?? item;
      if (!plane?.normal || typeof plane.constant !== "number") continue;
      const n = plane.normal.clone().normalize();
      const loc = n.clone().multiplyScalar(-plane.constant);
      out.push({
        location: [loc.x, loc.y, loc.z],
        direction: [n.x, n.y, n.z],
      });
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[section] getSectionPlanes failed", e);
  }
  return out;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// Restore planes (best-effort against SDK API drift)
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
export function setSectionPlanes(planes: SectionPlaneState[] | undefined | null): void {
  if (!planes || !planes.length) return;
  const clipper: any = ctx.clipper;
  if (!clipper) return;

  try {
    if (typeof clipper.deleteAll === "function") clipper.deleteAll();
  } catch {
    /* */
  }

  // Attempt API 1: createFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint
  const tryCreateFromNormal = (): boolean => {
    if (typeof clipper.createFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint !== "function") return false;
    try {
      for (const p of planes) {
        const n = new THREE.Vector3(p.direction[0], p.direction[1], p.direction[2]);
        const loc = new THREE.Vector3(p.location[0], p.location[1], p.location[2]);
        clipper.createFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(ctx.world, n, loc);
      }
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  // Attempt API 2: create(world) then adjust item's plane
  const tryCreateInteractive = (): boolean => {
    if (typeof clipper.create !== "function") return false;
    try {
      for (const p of planes) {
        const item: any = clipper.create(ctx.world);
        const plane: THREE.Plane | undefined =
          item?.three?.plane ?? item?.plane ?? item?.three;
        if (plane?.normal) {
          plane.normal.set(p.direction[0], p.direction[1], p.direction[2]);
          plane.constant = -new THREE.Vector3(
            p.location[0],
            p.location[1],
            p.location[2],
          ).dot(plane.normal);
        }
      }
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  };

  if (!tryCreateFromNormal()) {
    if (!tryCreateInteractive()) {
      console.warn(
        "[section] could not restore planes â€” SDK exposes neither createFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint nor create",
      );
    }
  }
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// Setup
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
// resetSection -- clear all planes, disable mode, uncheck the panel toggle
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
export function resetSection(): number {
  let removed = 0;
  try {
    const clipper: any = ctx.clipper;
    if (clipper) {
      removed = getSectionPlanes().length;
      if (typeof clipper.deleteAll === "function") clipper.deleteAll();
      if (clipper.list && typeof clipper.list.clear === "function") clipper.list.clear();
    }
  } catch (e) { console.warn("[section] reset deleteAll failed", e); }

  setSectionOn(false);

  try {
    const hosts: HTMLElement[] = [ctx.leftHost as any, document.body];
    for (const host of hosts) {
      if (!host) continue;
      const labels = Array.from(host.querySelectorAll("label")) as HTMLLabelElement[];
      for (const label of labels) {
        const txt = (label.textContent || "").toLowerCase();
        if (txt.includes("enable section")) {
          const input = label.querySelector('input[type="checkbox"]') as HTMLInputElement | null;
          if (input) input.checked = false;
        }
      }
      const el = host.querySelector("bim-checkbox[data-section]") as any;
      if (el) el.checked = false;
    }
    if (checkboxEl) checkboxEl.checked = false;
  } catch { /* */ }

  try { (ctx.features as any).sectionCutInteraction?.cancel?.(); } catch { /* */ }
  try { (ctx.features as any).sectionCutInteraction?.disable?.(); } catch { /* */ }
  try { (ctx.features as any).sectionBox?.disable?.(); } catch { /* */ }

  return removed;
}
export function setupSection() {
  ctx.rightHost.addEventListener("dblclick", async (e: MouseEvent) => {
    if (isDrawingActive() || isMeasureEnabled() || !enabled) return;

    const canvas = (ctx.world.renderer.three as THREE.WebGLRenderer).domElement;
    if (e.target !== canvas) return;

    const clipper: any = ctx.clipper;
    let created = false;

    try {
      const caster: any = (ctx.components as any).get(OBC.Raycasters).get(ctx.world);
      const hit: any = await caster.castRay();

      if (hit) {
        const pt = hit.point
          ? new THREE.Vector3(hit.point.x, hit.point.y, hit.point.z)
          : (hit.ray?.origin && hit.distance != null
              ? hit.ray.origin.clone().add(hit.ray.direction.clone().multiplyScalar(hit.distance))
              : null);

        let normal: THREE.Vector3 | null = null;
        const rawN = hit.normal ?? hit.face?.normal ?? null;
        if (rawN) {
          normal = new THREE.Vector3(rawN.x, rawN.y, rawN.z);
          if (hit.object && hit.object.matrixWorld) {
            try {
              const nm = new THREE.Matrix3().getNormalMatrix(hit.object.matrixWorld);
              normal.applyMatrix3(nm).normalize();
            } catch { normal.normalize(); }
          } else {
            normal.normalize();
          }
        }

        if (pt && normal) {
          // Flip toward camera so "front" always faces the user
          const camDir = new THREE.Vector3();
          (ctx.world.camera.three as THREE.Camera).getWorldDirection(camDir);
          if (normal.dot(camDir) > 0) normal.negate();

          if (typeof clipper.createFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint === "function") {
            clipper.createFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(ctx.world, normal, pt);
            created = true;
          } else if (typeof clipper.create === "function") {
            const item: any = clipper.create(ctx.world);
            const plane: THREE.Plane | undefined =
              item?.three?.plane ?? item?.plane ?? item?.three;
            if (plane?.normal) {
              plane.normal.copy(normal);
              plane.constant = -pt.dot(normal);
              created = true;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("[section] face-aligned create failed", err);
    }

    if (!created) {
      try { clipper.create(ctx.world); }
      catch (err) { console.warn("[section] fallback create failed", err); }
    }
  });
  ctx.features.section = {
    isEnabled: isSectionOn,
    toggle: toggleSection,
    getPlanes: getSectionPlanes,
    setPlanes: setSectionPlanes,
    reset: resetSection,
    deleteAllPlanes: resetSection,
  };
  (window as any).__obsSection = {
    reset: resetSection,
    getPlanes: getSectionPlanes,
    toggle: toggleSection,
    isEnabled: isSectionOn,
    setOn: setSectionOn,
  };
}
// ============================================================================
// Section visual auto-hide + grid toggle
// ============================================================================
let autoHideSectionVisuals = true;

export function hideSectionVisuals(): number {
  let count = 0;
  try {
    const clipper: any = ctx.clipper;
    if (!clipper) return 0;
    if ("visible" in clipper) {
      try { clipper.visible = false; count++; } catch { /* */ }
    }
    const list: any = clipper.list;
    if (list) {
      const iterable: any = list[Symbol.iterator] ? list : null;
      if (iterable) {
        for (const entry of iterable) {
          const item = Array.isArray(entry) ? entry[1] : entry;
          const three = item?.three;
          if (!three) continue;
          try { three.visible = false; count++; } catch { /* */ }
          try {
            const mat: any = three.material;
            if (mat) {
              mat.opacity = 0;
              mat.transparent = true;
              mat.depthWrite = false;
              mat.needsUpdate = true;
            }
          } catch { /* */ }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { console.warn("[section] hideSectionVisuals", e); }
  return count;
}

export function showSectionVisuals(): number {
  let count = 0;
  try {
    const clipper: any = ctx.clipper;
    if (!clipper) return 0;
    if ("visible" in clipper) {
      try { clipper.visible = true; count++; } catch { /* */ }
    }
    const list: any = clipper.list;
    if (list) {
      const iterable: any = list[Symbol.iterator] ? list : null;
      if (iterable) {
        for (const entry of iterable) {
          const item = Array.isArray(entry) ? entry[1] : entry;
          const three = item?.three;
          if (!three) continue;
          try { three.visible = true; count++; } catch { /* */ }
          try {
            const mat: any = three.material;
            if (mat) {
              mat.opacity = 0.5;
              mat.transparent = true;
              mat.needsUpdate = true;
            }
          } catch { /* */ }
        }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { console.warn("[section] showSectionVisuals", e); }
  return count;
}

export function setAutoHideSectionVisuals(on: boolean): void {
  autoHideSectionVisuals = on;
  if (!on) showSectionVisuals();
  else hideSectionVisuals();
}

(function autoHideLoop() {
  setInterval(() => {
    if (!autoHideSectionVisuals) return;
    try {
      const clipper: any = ctx.clipper;
      if (!clipper) return;
      const list: any = clipper.list;
      if (!list) return;
      const iterable: any = list[Symbol.iterator] ? list : null;
      if (!iterable) return;
      let anyVisible = false;
      for (const entry of iterable) {
        const item = Array.isArray(entry) ? entry[1] : entry;
        const three = item?.three;
        if (three && three.visible !== false) { anyVisible = true; break; }
      }
      if (anyVisible) hideSectionVisuals();
    } catch { /* */ }
  }, 500);
})();

let gridVisible = true;

function applyGridVisible(on: boolean): void {
  gridVisible = on;
  try {
    const grid: any = (ctx as any).grid;
    const grids: any = (ctx as any).grids;
    if (grid) {
      const target = grid.three ?? grid.helper ?? grid.object ?? grid;
      if (target && "visible" in target) target.visible = on;
    }
    if (grids && grids.list) {
      const iterable: any = grids.list[Symbol.iterator] ? grids.list : null;
      if (iterable) for (const entry of iterable) {
        const g = Array.isArray(entry) ? entry[1] : entry;
        if (g?.three && "visible" in g.three) g.three.visible = on;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) { console.warn("[section] grid toggle", e); }
  try {
    const btn = document.getElementById("obs-grid-toggle");
    if (btn) {
      btn.textContent = on ? "Grid ON" : "Grid OFF";
      (btn as HTMLElement).style.background = on
        ? "rgba(20,22,30,0.85)" : "rgba(60,20,20,0.85)";
    }
  } catch { /* */ }
}

export function setupGridToggle(): void {
  if ((window as any).__obsGridInstalled) return;
  (window as any).__obsGridInstalled = true;

  (window as any).__obsGrid = {
    show: () => applyGridVisible(true),
    hide: () => applyGridVisible(false),
    toggle: () => applyGridVisible(!gridVisible),
    isVisible: () => gridVisible,
  };

  try {
    const sec: any = (window as any).__obsSection ?? {};
    sec.hideVisuals = hideSectionVisuals;
    sec.showVisuals = showSectionVisuals;
    sec.setAutoHideVisuals = setAutoHideSectionVisuals;
    (window as any).__obsSection = sec;
  } catch { /* */ }

  try {
    const btn = document.createElement("button");
    btn.id = "obs-grid-toggle";
    btn.type = "button";
    btn.title = "Toggle ground grid (G)";
    btn.textContent = "Grid ON";
    btn.style.cssText =
      "position:absolute;bottom:16px;right:16px;z-index:60;" +
      "padding:6px 12px;background:rgba(20,22,30,0.85);color:#e8eaf0;" +
      "border:1px solid #2a2e3a;border-radius:6px;" +
      "font:600 11px system-ui;cursor:pointer;user-select:none";
    btn.addEventListener("click", () => applyGridVisible(!gridVisible));
    const host = (ctx.rightHost as HTMLElement) ?? document.getElementById("viewport") ?? document.body;
    if (!host) { console.warn("[section] no host for grid button, retrying later"); return; }
    host.appendChild(btn);
  } catch (e) { console.warn("[section] grid button", e); }

  window.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey) return;
    if (e.key !== "g" && e.key !== "G") return;
    const t = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
    if (t && t.closest && t.closest("input,textarea,select,[contenteditable]")) return;
    applyGridVisible(!gridVisible);
    e.preventDefault();
  });

  console.log("[section] grid toggle ready (button bottom-right, key G)");
}

(function bootGridToggle() {
  let tries = 0;
  const timer = setInterval(() => {
    tries++;
    const hasGrid = !!(ctx as any).grid || !!(ctx as any).grids;
    if (hasGrid || tries > 120) {
      clearInterval(timer);
      if (hasGrid) setupGridToggle();
    }
  }, 250);
})();
// ============================================================================
// Hide ALL section visual planes (clipper + section-box + cut-interaction)
// ============================================================================
function hideAllSectionVisuals(): number {
  let n = 0;
  try {
    const scene = (ctx as any)?.world?.scene?.three;
    if (scene) {
      scene.traverse((obj: any) => {
        const name = String(obj?.name ?? "").toLowerCase();
        const cls = String(obj?.constructor?.name ?? "").toLowerCase();
        const isSectionLike =
          name.includes("section") || name.includes("clip") ||
          name.includes("cut") || name.includes("box") ||
          cls.includes("section") || cls.includes("clip");
        if (!isSectionLike) return;
        if ("visible" in obj) { obj.visible = false; n++; }
        const mat: any = obj.material;
        if (mat) {
          if (Array.isArray(mat)) mat.forEach((m: any) => { m.opacity = 0; m.transparent = true; });
          else { mat.opacity = 0; mat.transparent = true; }
        }
      });
    }
  } catch (e) { console.warn("[section] hideAllSectionVisuals", e); }

  // Also hide any direct section-box helpers
  try {
    const sb: any = (ctx.features as any).sectionBox;
    if (sb) {
      if (typeof sb.hideVisual === "function") { sb.hideVisual(); n++; }
      if (typeof sb.setVisible === "function") { sb.setVisible(false); n++; }
    }
  } catch { /* */ }

  // And the section-cut-interaction plane
  try {
    const sci: any = (ctx.features as any).sectionCutInteraction;
    if (sci && typeof sci.hidePlane === "function") { sci.hidePlane(); n++; }
    if (sci && typeof sci.setVisible === "function") { sci.setVisible(false); n++; }
  } catch { /* */ }

  return n;
}

(function autoHideAll() {
  setInterval(() => { hideAllSectionVisuals(); }, 400);
})();

try {
  const sec: any = (window as any).__obsSection ?? {};
  sec.hideAll = hideAllSectionVisuals;
  (window as any).__obsSection = sec;
} catch { /* */ }

===== FILE: src/features/gizmo.ts =====
/**
 * Transform gizmo (Blender/3ds Max class).
 *
 * Modes: move | rotate | scale | none
 *
 * Two independent target channels:
 *   â€¢ Fragment selection (default)
 *   â€¢ Override provider (used by authoring â€” authored elements)
 *   â€¢ Edit targets (used by frag-edit â€” model element clones)
 *
 * Priority at refresh(): editTargets > overrideProvider > fragment selection.
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";
import { inputRouter, type Gesture } from "../core/input-router";
import { selection } from "./selection";

export type GizmoMode = "move" | "rotate" | "scale" | "none";

export interface GizmoCallbacks {
  onStart?: () => void;
  onUpdate?: (delta: THREE.Vector3, axis: "x" | "y" | "z" | "free") => void;
  onEnd?: (delta: THREE.Vector3, axis: "x" | "y" | "z" | "free") => void;
}

export interface GizmoTarget {
  center: THREE.Vector3;
  box: THREE.Box3;
}

const AXIS_COLOR = { x: 0xef4444, y: 0x22c55e, z: 0x3b82f6, free: 0xfbbf24 };

class TransformGizmo {
  private mode: GizmoMode = "none";
  private root: THREE.Group;
  private moveGroup: THREE.Group;
  private rotateGroup: THREE.Group;
  private scaleGroup: THREE.Group;
  private host: HTMLElement | null = null;
  private unsubGesture: (() => void) | null = null;
  private unsubHover: (() => void) | null = null;

  private dragging = false;
  private activeAxis: "x" | "y" | "z" | "free" | null = null;
  private dragStartValue: THREE.Vector3 | null = null;
  private hovered: THREE.Object3D | null = null;

  private basePos = new THREE.Vector3();
  private baseScale = 1;

  private cb: GizmoCallbacks = {};
  private editCb: GizmoCallbacks = {};
  private overrideProvider: (() => Promise<GizmoTarget | null>) | null = null;
  private editTargets: THREE.Object3D[] = [];

  private ray = new THREE.Raycaster();
  private ndc = new THREE.Vector2();

  constructor() {
    this.root = new THREE.Group();
    this.root.name = "obs-gizmo";
    this.moveGroup = new THREE.Group();
    this.rotateGroup = new THREE.Group();
    this.scaleGroup = new THREE.Group();
    this.root.add(this.moveGroup, this.rotateGroup, this.scaleGroup);
    this.buildMove();
    this.buildRotate();
    this.buildScale();
    this.root.visible = false;
  }

  private mkMat(color: number, opacity = 1) {
    return new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({
      color, transparent: opacity < 1, opacity,
      depthTest: false, depthWrite: false, side: THREE.DoubleSide,
    });
  }

  private buildMove() {
    const len = 1.2, shaftR = 0.02, headR = 0.09, headL = 0.22;
    const axes = [
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), color: AXIS_COLOR.x },
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), color: AXIS_COLOR.y },
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), color: AXIS_COLOR.z },
    ];
    for (const { dir, color } of axes) {
      const g = new THREE.Group();
      const shaft = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(shaftR, shaftR, len, 8), this.mkMat(color));
      shaft.position.copy(dir.clone().multiplyScalar(len / 2));
      const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(headR, headL, 12), this.mkMat(color));
      head.position.copy(dir.clone().multiplyScalar(len));
      const q = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), dir.clone().normalize());
      shaft.quaternion.copy(q); head.quaternion.copy(q);
      const ax = dir.x ? "x" : dir.y ? "y" : "z";
      shaft.userData.axis = ax; head.userData.axis = ax;
      shaft.renderOrder = 100; head.renderOrder = 100;
      g.add(shaft, head);
      this.moveGroup.add(g);
    }
    const center = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.18, 0.18, 0.18), this.mkMat(AXIS_COLOR.free, 0.85));
    center.userData.axis = "free";
    center.renderOrder = 100;
    this.moveGroup.add(center);
  }

  private buildRotate() {
    const axes = [
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), color: AXIS_COLOR.y },
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), color: AXIS_COLOR.x },
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), color: AXIS_COLOR.z },
    ];
    for (const { dir, color } of axes) {
      const g = new THREE.Group();
      const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(1.0, 0.015, 8, 64), this.mkMat(color, 0.9));
      ring.quaternion.setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), dir.clone().normalize());
      ring.userData.axis = dir.x ? "x" : dir.y ? "y" : "z";
      ring.renderOrder = 100;
      g.add(ring);
      this.rotateGroup.add(g);
    }
  }

  private buildScale() {
    const axes = [
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0), color: AXIS_COLOR.x },
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), color: AXIS_COLOR.y },
      { dir: new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, 1), color: AXIS_COLOR.z },
    ];
    for (const { dir, color } of axes) {
      const g = new THREE.Group();
      const shaft = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.015, 0.015, 1.0, 6), this.mkMat(color));
      shaft.position.copy(dir.clone().multiplyScalar(0.5));
      const head = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14, 0.14, 0.14), this.mkMat(color));
      head.position.copy(dir.clone().multiplyScalar(1.0));
      const q = new THREE.Quaternion().setFromUnitVectors(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), dir.clone().normalize());
      shaft.quaternion.copy(q);
      const ax = dir.x ? "x" : dir.y ? "y" : "z";
      shaft.userData.axis = ax; head.userData.axis = ax;
      shaft.renderOrder = 100; head.renderOrder = 100;
      g.add(shaft, head);
      this.scaleGroup.add(g);
    }
    const uni = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.14, 0.14, 0.14), this.mkMat(AXIS_COLOR.free));
    uni.userData.axis = "free";
    uni.renderOrder = 100;
    this.scaleGroup.add(uni);
  }

  /* â”€â”€ Public API â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */
  setup(host: HTMLElement): void {
    this.host = host;
    ctx.world.scene.three.add(this.root);

    // Priority 100 â€” gizmo runs before everything else so its handle drag
    // always claims the gesture even when a modeling tool is active.
    this.unsubGesture = inputRouter.subscribe((g) => this.handleGesture(g), 100);
    this.unsubHover = inputRouter.subscribeHover((e) => this.handleHover(e));

    ctx.world.camera.controls.addEventListener("update", () => this.refreshScale());
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.unsubGesture?.();
    this.unsubHover?.();
    this.unsubGesture = null;
    this.unsubHover = null;
  }

  setMode(mode: GizmoMode): void {
    this.mode = mode;
    this.moveGroup.visible = mode === "move";
    this.rotateGroup.visible = mode === "rotate";
    this.scaleGroup.visible = mode === "scale";
    void this.refresh();
  }
  getMode(): GizmoMode { return this.mode; }

  setCallbacks(cb: GizmoCallbacks): void { this.cb = cb; }
  setEditCallbacks(cb: GizmoCallbacks): void { this.editCb = cb; }

  setOverrideProvider(provider: (() => Promise<GizmoTarget | null>) | null): void {
    this.overrideProvider = provider;
    void this.refresh();
  }
  getOverrideProvider(): (() => Promise<GizmoTarget | null>) | null {
    return this.overrideProvider;
  }

  /** Frag-edit channel. When non-empty, these objects win over everything else. */
  setEditTargets(objects: THREE.Object3D[]): void {
    this.editTargets = objects.slice();
    void this.refresh();
  }
  hasEditTargets(): boolean { return this.editTargets.length > 0; }

  private activeCallbacks(): GizmoCallbacks {
    return this.editTargets.length > 0 ? this.editCb : this.cb;
  }

  async refresh(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.mode === "none") { this.root.visible = false; return; }

    let center: THREE.Vector3 | null = null;
    const size = new THREE.Vector3(1, 1, 1);

    // Priority 1 â€” frag-edit targets
    if (this.editTargets.length > 0) {
      const box = new THREE.Box3();
      for (const o of this.editTargets) box.union(new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(o));
      if (box.isEmpty()) { this.root.visible = false; return; }
      center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
      box.getSize(size);
    }
    // Priority 2 â€” authoring override provider
    else if (this.overrideProvider) {
      const r = await this.overrideProvider();
      if (!r) { this.root.visible = false; return; }
      center = r.center.clone();
      r.box.getSize(size);
    }
    // Priority 3 â€” fragment selection
    else {
      if (selection.isEmpty()) { this.root.visible = false; return; }
      const box = await selection.boundingBox();
      if (!box) { this.root.visible = false; return; }
      center = box.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3());
      box.getSize(size);
    }

    this.basePos.copy(center);
    const diag = size.length() || 1;
    this.baseScale = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(diag / 4, 0.3, 5);
    this.root.position.copy(this.basePos);
    this.refreshScale();
    this.root.visible = true;
  }

  private refreshScale(): void {
    if (!this.root.visible) return;
    const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
    const dist = cam.position.distanceTo(this.root.position);
    const f = dist * 0.15;
    this.root.scale.setScalar(Math.max(0.2, f * this.baseScale));
  }

  isDragging(): boolean { return this.dragging; }

  /* â”€â”€ Interaction (via input-router) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */
  private handleGesture(g: Gesture): boolean {
    if (!this.root.visible) return false;

    if (g.kind === "drag-start") {
      if (g.button !== 0) return false;
      const picked = this.pickHandle(g.event);
      if (!picked) return false;
      this.dragging = true;
      this.activeAxis = (picked.userData.axis as any) ?? "free";
      const plane = this.dragPlane();
      const start = plane ? this.raycastPlane(g.event, plane) : null;
      this.dragStartValue = start ? start.clone() : null;
      this.activeCallbacks().onStart?.();
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-move" && this.dragging) {
      const plane = this.dragPlane();
      if (plane) {
        const hit = this.raycastPlane(g.event, plane);
        if (hit && this.dragStartValue) {
          const delta = this.constrainDelta(hit.clone().sub(this.dragStartValue));
          this.activeCallbacks().onUpdate?.(delta, this.activeAxis ?? "free");
        }
      }
      return true;
    }

    if (g.kind === "drag-end" && this.dragging) {
      const plane = this.dragPlane();
      const end = plane ? this.raycastPlane(g.event, plane) : null;
      let delta = new THREE.Vector3();
      if (end && this.dragStartValue) delta = end.clone().sub(this.dragStartValue);
      const axis = this.activeAxis ?? "free";
      const finalDelta = this.constrainDelta(delta);
      this.dragging = false;
      this.activeAxis = null;
      this.dragStartValue = null;
      this.activeCallbacks().onEnd?.(finalDelta, axis);
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  private handleHover(e: PointerEvent): void {
    if (!this.root.visible || this.dragging) return;
    this.updateHover(e);
  }

  /* â”€â”€ Internals â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */
  private updateNdc(e: PointerEvent) {
    const r = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
    this.ndc.set(((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width) * 2 - 1, -((e.clientY - r.top) / r.height) * 2 + 1);
  }

  private pickHandle(e: PointerEvent): THREE.Object3D | null {
    this.updateNdc(e);
    this.ray.setFromCamera(this.ndc, ctx.world.camera.three);
    const group = this.mode === "move" ? this.moveGroup : this.mode === "rotate" ? this.rotateGroup : this.scaleGroup;
    const hits = this.ray.intersectObjects(group.children, true);
    if (!hits.length) return null;
    let o: THREE.Object3D | null = hits[0].object;
    while (o && !o.userData.axis) o = o.parent;
    return o;
  }

  private updateHover(e: PointerEvent): void {
    const picked = this.pickHandle(e);
    if (picked === this.hovered) return;
    if (this.hovered) this.setHandleHighlight(this.hovered, false);
    this.hovered = picked;
    if (picked) this.setHandleHighlight(picked, true);
    if (this.host) this.host.style.cursor = picked ? "grab" : "";
  }

  private setHandleHighlight(obj: THREE.Object3D, on: boolean): void {
    obj.traverse((m: any) => {
      if (!m.isMesh) return;
      const mat = m.material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
      if (!mat?.color) return;
      if (on) mat.color.offsetHSL(0, 0, 0.25); else mat.color.offsetHSL(0, 0, -0.25);
    });
  }

  private dragPlane(): THREE.Plane | null {
    if (!this.activeAxis) return null;
    const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
    const camDir = new THREE.Vector3();
    cam.getWorldDirection(camDir);
    if (this.activeAxis === "free") {
      return new THREE.Plane().setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(camDir, this.basePos);
    }
    const axis = new THREE.Vector3(
      this.activeAxis === "x" ? 1 : 0,
      this.activeAxis === "y" ? 1 : 0,
      this.activeAxis === "z" ? 1 : 0,
    );
    const normal = new THREE.Vector3().crossVectors(axis, camDir).cross(axis);
    if (normal.lengthSq() < 1e-6) normal.copy(camDir);
    normal.normalize();
    return new THREE.Plane().setFromNormalAndCoplanarPoint(normal, this.basePos);
  }

  private raycastPlane(e: PointerEvent, plane: THREE.Plane): THREE.Vector3 | null {
    this.updateNdc(e);
    this.ray.setFromCamera(this.ndc, ctx.world.camera.three);
    const p = new THREE.Vector3();
    const hit = this.ray.ray.intersectPlane(plane, p);
    return hit ? p : null;
  }

  private constrainDelta(delta: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
    if (!this.activeAxis || this.activeAxis === "free") return delta;
    if (this.activeAxis === "x") return new THREE.Vector3(delta.x, 0, 0);
    if (this.activeAxis === "y") return new THREE.Vector3(0, delta.y, 0);
    return new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, delta.z);
  }
}

export const gizmo = new TransformGizmo();

===== FILE: src/features/snapping.ts =====
/**
 * Object snap system (AutoCAD / Revit class).
 *
 * Each snap type has:
 *   - A priority level. Higher priority wins over a closer low-priority
 *     snap â€” same as Revit. Endpoint (100) beats Nearest (55) even if
 *     Nearest is a few pixels closer.
 *   - A distinctive SVG marker drawn at the snapped point.
 *   - A short label shown next to the marker.
 *
 * Snaps are gated by the current tool â€” dims prefer endpoints/corners/faces;
 * wall placement prefers grid/endpoint/midpoint.
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";

export type SnapType =
  | "endpoint"
  | "intersection"
  | "corner"
  | "midpoint"
  | "center"
  | "perpendicular"
  | "tangent"
  | "quadrant"
  | "vertex"
  | "face"
  | "nearest"
  | "parallel"
  | "extension"
  | "grid";

export interface SnapResult {
  point: THREE.Vector3;
  type: SnapType;
  screenDist: number;
}

/** Priority table â€” higher wins. Matches Revit's hierarchy. */
const SNAP_PRIORITY: Record<SnapType, number> = {
  endpoint:      100,
  intersection:   95,
  corner:         92,
  midpoint:       90,
  center:         85,
  perpendicular:  80,
  tangent:        75,
  quadrant:       70,
  vertex:         65,
  face:           60,
  nearest:        55,
  parallel:       45,
  extension:      40,
  grid:           15,
};

const ALL_TYPES: SnapType[] = [
  "endpoint", "intersection", "corner", "midpoint", "center",
  "perpendicular", "tangent", "quadrant", "vertex", "face",
  "nearest", "parallel", "extension", "grid",
];

/* Revit-style SVG markers â€” 32Ã—32 viewBox. */
const SVG_MARKER: Record<SnapType, string> = {
  endpoint:
    `<rect x="9" y="9" width="14" height="14" fill="#F59E0B" stroke="#0B0D12" stroke-width="2"/>`,
  intersection:
    `<path d="M6 6 L26 26 M26 6 L6 26" stroke="#EF4444" stroke-width="3" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
  corner:
    `<path d="M8 8 L8 24 L24 24" stroke="#FBBF24" stroke-width="3" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>` +
    `<circle cx="8" cy="8" r="2.5" fill="#FBBF24"/>`,
  midpoint:
    `<polygon points="16,8 26,24 6,24" fill="#22C55E" stroke="#0B0D12" stroke-width="2" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`,
  center:
    `<circle cx="16" cy="16" r="9" fill="none" stroke="#38BDF8" stroke-width="2.5"/>` +
    `<circle cx="16" cy="16" r="2.4" fill="#38BDF8"/>`,
  perpendicular:
    `<path d="M8 8 L8 26 L26 26" stroke="#C084FC" stroke-width="3" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>` +
    `<rect x="8" y="20" width="6" height="6" fill="#C084FC"/>`,
  tangent:
    `<circle cx="18" cy="18" r="8" fill="none" stroke="#F472B6" stroke-width="2.5"/>` +
    `<line x1="3" y1="10" x2="29" y2="10" stroke="#F472B6" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
  quadrant:
    `<circle cx="16" cy="16" r="9" fill="none" stroke="#34D399" stroke-width="2"/>` +
    `<circle cx="16" cy="7" r="2" fill="#34D399"/>` +
    `<circle cx="25" cy="16" r="2" fill="#34D399"/>` +
    `<circle cx="16" cy="25" r="2" fill="#34D399"/>` +
    `<circle cx="7" cy="16" r="2" fill="#34D399"/>`,
  vertex:
    `<rect x="11" y="11" width="10" height="10" fill="#38BDF8" stroke="#0B0D12" stroke-width="2" transform="rotate(45 16 16)"/>`,
  face:
    `<rect x="8" y="8" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="#38BDF8" stroke-width="2"/>` +
    `<circle cx="16" cy="16" r="2.6" fill="#38BDF8"/>`,
  nearest:
    `<circle cx="16" cy="16" r="4" fill="#F59E0B"/>` +
    `<circle cx="16" cy="16" r="9" fill="none" stroke="#F59E0B" stroke-width="1.5" opacity="0.55"/>`,
  parallel:
    `<path d="M8 12 H24 M8 20 H24" stroke="#A78BFA" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round"/>`,
  extension:
    `<path d="M4 16 H28 M22 10 L28 16 L22 22" stroke="#F472B6" stroke-width="2.5" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/>`,
  grid:
    `<path d="M6 16 H26 M16 6 V26" stroke="#38BDF8" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round"/>` +
    `<circle cx="16" cy="16" r="3" fill="none" stroke="#38BDF8" stroke-width="1.5"/>`,
};

const SVG_LABEL: Record<SnapType, string> = {
  endpoint:       "End",
  intersection:   "Int",
  corner:         "Corner",
  midpoint:       "Mid",
  center:         "Cen",
  perpendicular:  "Perp",
  tangent:        "Tan",
  quadrant:       "Quad",
  vertex:         "Vtx",
  face:           "Face",
  nearest:        "Near",
  parallel:       "Par",
  extension:      "Ext",
  grid:           "Grid",
};

type AuthoredLite = {
  id: string;
  type: string;
  params: {
    start?: [number, number];
    end?: [number, number];
    point?: [number, number];
    polygon?: [number, number][];
    elevation?: number;
    height?: number;
    width?: number;
    depth?: number;
    thickness?: number;
  };
};

function getAuthoredSafe(): AuthoredLite[] {
  try {
    const mod = (window as any).__obsAuthoring;
    if (mod?.getElements) return mod.getElements() as AuthoredLite[];
  } catch { /* */ }
  try {
    const auth = (ctx.features as any)?.authoring;
    if (auth?.getElements) return auth.getElements();
  } catch { /* */ }
  return [];
}

class SnapManager {
  private enabled = true;
  private types: Set<SnapType> = new Set(ALL_TYPES);
  private gridSize = 0.1;
  private pixelTolerance = 16;
  private workPlaneY = 0;

  private orthoOn = false;
  private orthoBase: THREE.Vector3 | null = null;
  private orthoAxis: "x" | "y" | "z" | null = null;

  private overlay: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
  private marker: HTMLDivElement | null = null;
  private label: HTMLDivElement | null = null;

  private lastPointer = { x: 0, y: 0 };

  setEnabled(on: boolean) {
    this.enabled = on;
    if (!on) this.hideIndicator();
  }
  isEnabled() { return this.enabled; }

  setGridSize(m: number) { this.gridSize = Math.max(0.001, m); }
  getGridSize() { return this.gridSize; }
  setPixelTolerance(px: number) { this.pixelTolerance = Math.max(4, px); }
  getPixelTolerance() { return this.pixelTolerance; }
  setWorkPlaneY(y: number) { this.workPlaneY = y; }
  getWorkPlaneY() { return this.workPlaneY; }

  setTypes(types: SnapType[]) { this.types = new Set(types); }
  getTypes(): SnapType[] { return [...this.types]; }
  getAllTypes(): SnapType[] { return [...ALL_TYPES]; }

  toggleType(t: SnapType, on?: boolean) {
    const enable = on ?? !this.types.has(t);
    if (enable) this.types.add(t);
    else this.types.delete(t);
  }
  enableAllTypes() { this.types = new Set(ALL_TYPES); }
  disableAllTypes() { this.types.clear(); }

  setOrtho(on: boolean, base: THREE.Vector3 | null, axis: "x" | "y" | "z" | null = null) {
    this.orthoOn = on;
    this.orthoBase = base ? base.clone() : null;
    this.orthoAxis = axis;
  }
  clearOrtho() {
    this.orthoOn = false;
    this.orthoBase = null;
    this.orthoAxis = null;
  }
  isOrtho() { return this.orthoOn; }

  private ensureOverlay() {
    if (this.overlay) return;
    try {
      const host = ctx.rightHost;
      if (!host) return;
      this.overlay = document.createElement("div");
      this.overlay.style.cssText =
        "position:absolute;inset:0;pointer-events:none;z-index:22;overflow:hidden";
      this.marker = document.createElement("div");
      this.marker.style.cssText =
        "position:absolute;width:32px;height:32px;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);display:none";
      this.label = document.createElement("div");
      this.label.style.cssText =
        "position:absolute;transform:translate(-50%,calc(-100% - 18px));" +
        "font:700 10px system-ui,sans-serif;color:#0B0D12;background:#FBBF24;" +
        "padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;white-space:nowrap;display:none;" +
        "box-shadow:0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.35);letter-spacing:.2px";
      this.overlay.append(this.marker, this.label);
      host.appendChild(this.overlay);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[snap] overlay init failed", e);
    }
  }

  showIndicator(result: SnapResult | null) {
    try {
      this.ensureOverlay();
      if (!result || !this.marker || !this.label || !ctx.world?.camera?.three) {
        if (this.marker) this.marker.style.display = "none";
        if (this.label) this.label.style.display = "none";
        return;
      }
      const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
      const p = result.point.clone().project(cam);
      const rect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
      const x = (p.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.width;
      const y = (-p.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.height;
      this.marker.innerHTML = `<svg width="32" height="32" viewBox="0 0 32 32">${SVG_MARKER[result.type]}</svg>`;
      this.marker.style.left = `${x}px`;
      this.marker.style.top = `${y}px`;
      this.marker.style.display = "block";
      this.label.textContent = SVG_LABEL[result.type];
      // Match label color to marker color
      const colorByType: Partial<Record<SnapType, string>> = {
        endpoint: "#F59E0B",
        intersection: "#EF4444",
        corner: "#FBBF24",
        midpoint: "#22C55E",
        center: "#38BDF8",
        perpendicular: "#C084FC",
        tangent: "#F472B6",
        quadrant: "#34D399",
        vertex: "#38BDF8",
        face: "#38BDF8",
        nearest: "#F59E0B",
        parallel: "#A78BFA",
        extension: "#F472B6",
        grid: "#38BDF8",
      };
      this.label.style.background = colorByType[result.type] ?? "#FBBF24";
      this.label.style.left = `${x}px`;
      this.label.style.top = `${y}px`;
      this.label.style.display = "block";
    } catch { /* never crash the modeling loop */ }
  }

  hideIndicator() { this.showIndicator(null); }

  /** Best snap for a pointer event. Priority-aware, never throws. */
  async query(e: MouseEvent, cursorWorld: THREE.Vector3 | null): Promise<SnapResult | null> {
    try {
      if (!this.enabled) return null;
      this.lastPointer = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
      const host = ctx.rightHost;
      if (!host || !ctx.world?.camera?.three) return null;
      const rect = host.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (rect.width < 1 || rect.height < 1) return null;

      const ndc = new THREE.Vector2(
        ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * 2 - 1,
        -((e.clientY - rect.top) / rect.height) * 2 + 1,
      );

      const candidates: SnapResult[] = [];
      const add = (point: THREE.Vector3, type: SnapType) => {
        const px = this.screenDistToPointer(point, rect, e.clientX, e.clientY);
        if (px <= this.pixelTolerance) {
          candidates.push({ point: point.clone(), type, screenDist: px });
        }
      };

      // Grid snap
      if (this.types.has("grid") && cursorWorld) {
        const g = this.gridSize;
        const gp = new THREE.Vector3(
          Math.round(cursorWorld.x / g) * g,
          this.workPlaneY,
          Math.round(cursorWorld.z / g) * g,
        );
        add(gp, "grid");
      }

      // Authored element snaps (endpoint, midpoint, center, corner, face, quadrant)
      const authored = getAuthoredSafe();
      for (const el of authored) {
        this.collectAuthoredSnaps(el, add, authored);
      }

      // Raycast â€” nearest + vertex
      if (this.types.has("nearest") || this.types.has("vertex") || this.types.has("face")) {
        try {
          const ray = new THREE.Raycaster();
          ray.setFromCamera(ndc, ctx.world.camera.three);
          const targets: THREE.Object3D[] = [];
          if (ctx.groundPlane) targets.push(ctx.groundPlane);
          if (ctx.fragments?.list) {
            for (const [, model] of ctx.fragments.list) {
              if (model?.object) targets.push(model.object);
            }
          }
          if (targets.length) {
            const hits = ray.intersectObjects(targets, true);
            if (hits.length && this.types.has("nearest")) {
              add(hits[0].point, "nearest");
            }
          }
        } catch { /* raycast can fail on disposed geometry */ }
      }

      // Ortho constraint â€” project candidates onto the axis
      if (this.orthoOn && this.orthoBase) {
        for (const c of candidates) {
          const d = c.point.clone().sub(this.orthoBase);
          if (this.orthoAxis === "x") {
            c.point.set(this.orthoBase.x + d.x, this.orthoBase.y, this.orthoBase.z);
          } else if (this.orthoAxis === "y") {
            c.point.set(this.orthoBase.x, this.orthoBase.y + d.y, this.orthoBase.z);
          } else if (this.orthoAxis === "z") {
            c.point.set(this.orthoBase.x, this.orthoBase.y, this.orthoBase.z + d.z);
          } else {
            const ax = Math.abs(d.x), ay = Math.abs(d.y), az = Math.abs(d.z);
            if (ax >= ay && ax >= az) c.point.set(this.orthoBase.x + d.x, this.orthoBase.y, this.orthoBase.z);
            else if (ay >= az) c.point.set(this.orthoBase.x, this.orthoBase.y + d.y, this.orthoBase.z);
            else c.point.set(this.orthoBase.x, this.orthoBase.y, this.orthoBase.z + d.z);
          }
          c.screenDist = this.screenDistToPointer(c.point, rect, e.clientX, e.clientY);
        }
      }

      if (!candidates.length) return null;

      // Priority-aware sort â€” a higher-priority snap wins over a closer
      // lower-priority one, exactly like Revit.
      candidates.sort((a, b) => {
        const pa = SNAP_PRIORITY[a.type] ?? 0;
        const pb = SNAP_PRIORITY[b.type] ?? 0;
        if (pa !== pb) return pb - pa;
        return a.screenDist - b.screenDist;
      });

      return candidates[0];
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("[snap] query failed", err);
      return null;
    }
  }

  private collectAuthoredSnaps(
    el: AuthoredLite,
    add: (p: THREE.Vector3, t: SnapType) => void,
    all: readonly AuthoredLite[],
  ) {
    try {
      const y = el.params.elevation ?? this.workPlaneY;
      const p = el.params;

      if (p.start && p.end) {
        const a = new THREE.Vector3(p.start[0], y, p.start[1]);
        const b = new THREE.Vector3(p.end[0], y, p.end[1]);
        const dx = b.x - a.x;
        const dz = b.z - a.z;
        const L = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
        const ux = dx / L;
        const uz = dz / L;
        const nx = -uz;
        const nz = ux;

        if (this.types.has("endpoint")) { add(a, "endpoint"); add(b, "endpoint"); }

        if (this.types.has("midpoint")) {
          add(new THREE.Vector3((a.x + b.x) / 2, y, (a.z + b.z) / 2), "midpoint");
        }

        // Corner â€” this endpoint coincides with another element's endpoint
        if (this.types.has("corner")) {
          const tol = 0.2;
          for (const endpoint of [a, b]) {
            for (const other of all) {
              if (other.id === el.id) continue;
              const op = other.params;
              if (!op.start || !op.end) continue;
              const oy = op.elevation ?? this.workPlaneY;
              const os = new THREE.Vector3(op.start[0], oy, op.start[1]);
              const oe = new THREE.Vector3(op.end[0], oy, op.end[1]);
              if (endpoint.distanceTo(os) < tol || endpoint.distanceTo(oe) < tol) {
                add(endpoint, "corner");
                break;
              }
            }
          }
        }

        // Face â€” sample points along both faces of a wall-like element
        if (this.types.has("face")) {
          const half = Math.max(0.02, (p.thickness ?? 0.2) / 2);
          const faceTypes = new Set(["wall", "duct", "pipe", "cable-tray", "conduit", "beam", "strip"]);
          if (faceTypes.has(el.type)) {
            for (const t of [0.25, 0.5, 0.75]) {
              const cx = a.x + dx * t;
              const cz = a.z + dz * t;
              add(new THREE.Vector3(cx + nx * half, y, cz + nz * half), "face");
              add(new THREE.Vector3(cx - nx * half, y, cz - nz * half), "face");
            }
          }
        }

        // Tangent â€” only meaningful for arc-based elements; skip silently
        // for linear elements. Activate when arc elements exist.

        // Extension â€” points slightly beyond each end
        if (this.types.has("extension")) {
          add(a.clone().addScaledVector(new THREE.Vector3(ux, 0, uz), -0.5), "extension");
          add(b.clone().addScaledVector(new THREE.Vector3(ux, 0, uz), 0.5), "extension");
        }
      }

      if (p.point) {
        const c = new THREE.Vector3(p.point[0], y, p.point[1]);
        if (this.types.has("center") || this.types.has("endpoint")) add(c, "center");
        if (this.types.has("quadrant") && (p.width || p.depth)) {
          const hw = (p.width ?? p.depth ?? 0.3) / 2;
          const hd = (p.depth ?? p.width ?? 0.3) / 2;
          add(new THREE.Vector3(c.x + hw, y, c.z), "quadrant");
          add(new THREE.Vector3(c.x - hw, y, c.z), "quadrant");
          add(new THREE.Vector3(c.x, y, c.z + hd), "quadrant");
          add(new THREE.Vector3(c.x, y, c.z - hd), "quadrant");
        }
      }

      if (p.polygon && p.polygon.length >= 2) {
        for (let i = 0; i < p.polygon.length; i++) {
          const pt = p.polygon[i];
          const v = new THREE.Vector3(pt[0], y, pt[1]);
          if (this.types.has("vertex") || this.types.has("endpoint")) add(v, "vertex");
          if (this.types.has("midpoint")) {
            const n = p.polygon[(i + 1) % p.polygon.length];
            add(new THREE.Vector3((pt[0] + n[0]) / 2, y, (pt[1] + n[1]) / 2), "midpoint");
          }
        }
      }
    } catch { /* skip broken element */ }
  }

  private screenDistToPointer(
    p: THREE.Vector3,
    rect: DOMRect,
    clientX: number,
    clientY: number,
  ): number {
    try {
      const v = p.clone().project(ctx.world.camera.three);
      const sx = (v.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.width + rect.left;
      const sy = (-v.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * rect.height + rect.top;
      return Math.hypot(sx - clientX, sy - clientY);
    } catch { return 9999; }
  }
}

export const snapping = new SnapManager();

/* -- Snap settings panel (unchanged API) ------------------------------ */

let snapPanel: HTMLElement | null = null;
let snapPanelVisible = false;

export function toggleSnapSettings() {
  setSnapSettingsVisible(!snapPanelVisible);
}

export function isSnapSettingsVisible() { return snapPanelVisible; }

export function setSnapSettingsVisible(on: boolean) {
  snapPanelVisible = on;
  if (!snapPanel) buildSnapPanel();
  if (snapPanel) snapPanel.style.display = on ? "block" : "none";
  if (on) renderSnapPanel();
}

function buildSnapPanel() {
  snapPanel = document.createElement("div");
  snapPanel.style.cssText =
    "position:fixed;bottom:48px;left:12px;width:300px;z-index:8500;" +
    "background:#1e2430;color:#e2e8f0;border:1px solid #334155;border-radius:10px;" +
    "box-shadow:0 8px 32px rgba(0,0,0,.4);font:12px/1.4 system-ui,sans-serif;display:none";
  document.body.appendChild(snapPanel);
}

function renderSnapPanel() {
  if (!snapPanel) return;
  const types = snapping.getAllTypes();
  const active = new Set(snapping.getTypes());

  // Color chips matching the marker colors
  const colorByType: Record<string, string> = {
    endpoint: "#F59E0B", intersection: "#EF4444", corner: "#FBBF24",
    midpoint: "#22C55E", center: "#38BDF8", perpendicular: "#C084FC",
    tangent: "#F472B6", quadrant: "#34D399", vertex: "#38BDF8",
    face: "#38BDF8", nearest: "#F59E0B", parallel: "#A78BFA",
    extension: "#F472B6", grid: "#38BDF8",
  };

  const checkboxes = types.map((t) => {
    const on = active.has(t);
    const color = colorByType[t] ?? "#94a3b8";
    return (
      '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:4px 6px;border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer;' +
      (on ? "background:#0f172a;" : "background:transparent;") + '">' +
      '<input type="checkbox" data-snap="' + t + '" ' + (on ? "checked" : "") +
      ' style="accent-color:' + color + '"/>' +
      '<span style="display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:2px;background:' + color + ';flex:none"></span>' +
      '<span style="flex:1">' + SVG_LABEL_FOR_PANEL(t) + '</span>' +
      '</label>'
    );
  }).join("");

  snapPanel.innerHTML =
    '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:10px 12px;border-bottom:1px solid #334155;font-weight:600">' +
      '<span>Object Snaps</span>' +
      '<button type="button" id="obs-snap-close" style="background:none;border:none;color:#94a3b8;font-size:16px;cursor:pointer">Ã—</button>' +
    '</div>' +
    '<div style="padding:10px 12px">' +
      '<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:8px">' +
        '<button type="button" id="obs-snap-all" style="flex:1;background:#334155;border:1px solid #475569;color:#e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:4px;cursor:pointer">All</button>' +
        '<button type="button" id="obs-snap-none" style="flex:1;background:#334155;border:1px solid #475569;color:#e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:4px;cursor:pointer">None</button>' +
        '<button type="button" id="obs-snap-toggle" style="flex:1;background:' + (snapping.isEnabled() ? "#1d4ed8" : "#334155") + ';border:1px solid #475569;color:#e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:4px;cursor:pointer">' +
          (snapping.isEnabled() ? "ON" : "OFF") +
        '</button>' +
      '</div>' +
      '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:2px;margin-bottom:10px">' + checkboxes + '</div>' +
      '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8">' +
        'Grid size (m)' +
        '<input type="number" id="obs-snap-grid" value="' + snapping.getGridSize() + '" step="0.05" min="0.001" ' +
          'style="flex:1;background:#0f172a;border:1px solid #334155;color:#e2e8f0;border-radius:4px;padding:4px 6px"/>' +
      '</label>' +
      '<label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;margin-top:6px">' +
        'Tolerance (px)' +
        '<input type="number" id="obs-snap-tol" value="' + snapping.getPixelTolerance() + '" step="1" min="4" ' +
          'style="flex:1;background:#0f172a;border:1px solid #334155;color:#e2e8f0;border-radius:4px;padding:4px 6px"/>' +
      '</label>' +
      '<div style="margin-top:8px;padding-top:8px;border-top:1px solid #334155;font-size:10px;color:#64748b;line-height:1.4">' +
        'Higher priority wins over closer snaps. Endpoint / Intersection / Corner beat Nearest and Grid.' +
      '</div>' +
    '</div>';

  snapPanel.querySelector("#obs-snap-close")?.addEventListener("click", () => setSnapSettingsVisible(false));
  snapPanel.querySelector("#obs-snap-all")?.addEventListener("click", () => { snapping.enableAllTypes(); renderSnapPanel(); });
  snapPanel.querySelector("#obs-snap-none")?.addEventListener("click", () => { snapping.disableAllTypes(); renderSnapPanel(); });
  snapPanel.querySelector("#obs-snap-toggle")?.addEventListener("click", () => {
    snapping.setEnabled(!snapping.isEnabled());
    renderSnapPanel();
  });
  snapPanel.querySelectorAll("[data-snap]").forEach((el) => {
    el.addEventListener("change", () => {
      const t = (el as HTMLInputElement).dataset.snap as SnapType;
      snapping.toggleType(t, (el as HTMLInputElement).checked);
    });
  });
  snapPanel.querySelector("#obs-snap-grid")?.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
    snapping.setGridSize(Number((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value) || 0.1);
  });
  snapPanel.querySelector("#obs-snap-tol")?.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
    snapping.setPixelTolerance(Number((e.target as HTMLInputElement).value) || 16);
  });
}

function SVG_LABEL_FOR_PANEL(t: string): string {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    endpoint: "Endpoint", intersection: "Intersection", corner: "Corner",
    midpoint: "Midpoint", center: "Center", perpendicular: "Perpendicular",
    tangent: "Tangent", quadrant: "Quadrant", vertex: "Vertex",
    face: "Face", nearest: "Nearest", parallel: "Parallel",
    extension: "Extension", grid: "Grid",
  };
  return map[t] ?? t;
}

===== FILE: src/features/measure.ts =====
import * as THREE from "three";
import * as OBC from "@thatopen/components";
import * as FRAGS from "@thatopen/fragments";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";
import { isDrawingActive } from "./drawing";
import { setSectionOn } from "./section";

let measureCheckboxEl: any = null;
export function setMeasureCheckboxRef(el: any) { measureCheckboxEl = el; }
export function isMeasureEnabled() { return ctx.lengthMeasurer.enabled; }

const measureLabelLayer = document.createElement("div");
measureLabelLayer.style.cssText = "position:absolute;inset:0;pointer-events:none;overflow:hidden;z-index:20";

type MLabel = { el: HTMLDivElement; pos: THREE.Vector3 };
const measureLabels: MLabel[] = [];
const xyzGroup = new THREE.Group();
let pendingMeasurePts: THREE.Vector3[] = [];

function clearMeasureLabels() { for (const l of measureLabels) l.el.remove(); measureLabels.length = 0; }

function addMeasureLabel(text: string, pos: THREE.Vector3) {
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  el.textContent = text;
  el.style.cssText = `position:absolute;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);background:#0284c7;color:#fff;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:6px;font:600 12px system-ui,sans-serif;white-space:nowrap;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,.45);border:1px solid #38bdf8;`;
  measureLabelLayer.appendChild(el);
  measureLabels.push({ el, pos: pos.clone() });
  updateMeasureLabels();
}

function updateMeasureLabels() {
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const w = ctx.rightHost.clientWidth, h = ctx.rightHost.clientHeight;
  for (const { el, pos } of measureLabels) {
    const v = pos.clone().project(cam);
    if (v.z > 1 || v.z < -1) { el.style.display = "none"; continue; }
    el.style.display = "block";
    el.style.left = `${(v.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * w}px`;
    el.style.top = `${(-v.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * h}px`;
  }
}

export function setMeasureEnabled(on: boolean) {
  ctx.lengthMeasurer.enabled = on;
  ctx.lengthMeasurer.visible = true;
  ctx.highlighter.enabled = !on;
  if (on) setSectionOn(false);
  // Hide the floating label layer when the tool is off. The underlying
  // dimensions remain in the OBC measurer -- re-enabling restores them.
  try { measureLabelLayer.style.display = on ? "block" : "none"; } catch { /* */ }
  const el = measureCheckboxEl ?? ctx.leftHost.querySelector("bim-checkbox[data-measure]");
  if (el) el.checked = on;
}

export function toggleMeasure() { setMeasureEnabled(!ctx.lengthMeasurer.enabled); }

export function clearMeasurements() {
  try {
    const list = (ctx.lengthMeasurer as any).list;
    if (list?.clear) list.clear();
    else if (typeof (ctx.lengthMeasurer as any).deleteAll === "function") (ctx.lengthMeasurer as any).deleteAll();
  } catch (e) { console.warn("clear measurements", e); }
  clearXyzGraphics();
}

function clearXyzGraphics() {
  while (xyzGroup.children.length) {
    const ch = xyzGroup.children[0] as any;
    xyzGroup.remove(ch);
    ch.geometry?.dispose?.();
    if (Array.isArray(ch.material)) ch.material.forEach((m: any) => m.dispose?.());
    else ch.material?.dispose?.();
  }
  clearMeasureLabels();
  pendingMeasurePts = [];
}

function line3(a: THREE.Vector3, b: THREE.Vector3, color: number) {
  const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([a.clone(), b.clone()]);
  const l = new THREE.Line(g, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color, depthTest: false, transparent: true }));
  l.renderOrder = 1000;
  return l;
}

function ball(p: THREE.Vector3, color: number) {
  const m = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.04, 12, 12), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color, depthTest: false }));
  m.position.copy(p); m.renderOrder = 1001;
  return m;
}

function drawXyzDimension(a: THREE.Vector3, b: THREE.Vector3) {
  clearXyzGraphics(); clearMeasureLabels();
  const dx = b.x - a.x, dy = b.y - a.y, dz = b.z - a.z;
  const pX = new THREE.Vector3(b.x, a.y, a.z), pXY = new THREE.Vector3(b.x, b.y, a.z);
  if (Math.abs(dx) > 1e-6) { xyzGroup.add(line3(a, pX, 0xef4444)); addMeasureLabel(`X ${(Math.abs(dx) * 1000).toFixed(1)} mm`, a.clone().lerp(pX, 0.5)); }
  if (Math.abs(dy) > 1e-6) { xyzGroup.add(line3(pX, pXY, 0x22c55e)); addMeasureLabel(`Y ${(Math.abs(dy) * 1000).toFixed(1)} mm`, pX.clone().lerp(pXY, 0.5)); }
  if (Math.abs(dz) > 1e-6) { xyzGroup.add(line3(pXY, b, 0x3b82f6)); addMeasureLabel(`Z ${(Math.abs(dz) * 1000).toFixed(1)} mm`, pXY.clone().lerp(b, 0.5)); }
  xyzGroup.add(line3(a, b, 0x38bdf8));
  addMeasureLabel(`${(a.distanceTo(b) * 1000).toFixed(1)} mm`, a.clone().lerp(b, 0.5));
  xyzGroup.add(ball(a, 0xffffff)); xyzGroup.add(ball(b, 0xffffff));
}

// â”€â”€ Setup â”€â”€
export function setupMeasure() {
  const lm = ctx.lengthMeasurer;
  lm.world = ctx.world;
  lm.color = new THREE.Color("#38bdf8");
  lm.enabled = false;
  lm.visible = true;
  try { lm.units = "mm"; } catch { /* */ }
  try { (lm as any).snappings = [FRAGS.SnappingClass.POINT, FRAGS.SnappingClass.LINE]; } catch { /* */ }

  ctx.rightHost.appendChild(measureLabelLayer);
  ctx.world.scene.three.add(xyzGroup);
  ctx.world.camera.controls.addEventListener("update", () => updateMeasureLabels());

  try {
    (lm.list as any).onItemSet.add(async ({ value: dim }: any) => {
      try {
        let val: number | null = null;
        if (typeof dim?.getValue === "function") val = await dim.getValue();
        else if (typeof dim?.value === "number") val = dim.value;
        else if (typeof dim?.rawValue === "number") val = dim.rawValue;

        let mid = new THREE.Vector3();
        const line = dim?.line ?? dim?.three ?? dim?.mesh;
        if (dim?.start && dim?.end) mid.copy(dim.start).add(dim.end).multiplyScalar(0.5);
        else if (line?.geometry?.attributes?.position) {
          const p = line.geometry.attributes.position;
          const a = new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(p, 0);
          const b = new THREE.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(p, p.count - 1);
          mid.copy(a).add(b).multiplyScalar(0.5);
        }

        if (val != null && Number.isFinite(val)) {
          const units = (lm as any).units || "m";
          let display = val, suffix = units;
          if (units === "mm" && Math.abs(val) < 50) { display = val * 1000; suffix = "mm"; }
          addMeasureLabel(`${display.toFixed(1)} ${suffix}`, mid);
        }
      } catch (err) { console.warn("[measure] label failed", err); }
    });
  } catch { /* */ }

  ctx.rightHost.addEventListener("dblclick", async () => {
    if (isDrawingActive() || !ctx.lengthMeasurer.enabled) return;
    try { ctx.lengthMeasurer.create(); } catch { /* */ }
    try {
      const caster = ctx.components.get(OBC.Raycasters).get(ctx.world);
      const result: any = await caster.castRay({
        snappingClasses: [FRAGS.SnappingClass.POINT, FRAGS.SnappingClass.LINE],
      });
      if (result?.point) {
        const pt = (result.point as THREE.Vector3).clone();
        pendingMeasurePts.push(pt);
        xyzGroup.add(ball(pt, 0x22d3ee));
        if (pendingMeasurePts.length >= 2) { drawXyzDimension(pendingMeasurePts[0], pendingMeasurePts[1]); pendingMeasurePts = []; }
      }
    } catch (err) { console.warn("[measure] castRay error", err); }
  });

  window.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
    if (!ctx.lengthMeasurer.enabled) return;
    if (e.code === "Enter" || e.code === "NumpadEnter") { try { (ctx.lengthMeasurer as any).endCreation?.(); } catch { /* */ } }
    if (e.code === "Escape") { try { (ctx.lengthMeasurer as any).cancelCreation?.(); } catch { /* */ } }
  });

  ctx.features.measure = { isEnabled: isMeasureEnabled, toggle: toggleMeasure, clear: clearMeasurements };
}

===== FILE: src/features/box-selection.ts =====
/**
 * Revit-style 3D rubber-band selection (query only).
 * Gesture + overlay are owned by features/navigation.ts, which calls commit().
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";
import { selection } from "./selection";
import { setSelectedAuthored } from "./authoring";
import { getElementWorldBoxes } from "../modules/geometry/world-box";

export type BoxMode = "window" | "crossing";
export interface BoxRect {
  x1: number;
  y1: number;
  x2: number;
  y2: number;
}

const _corner = new THREE.Vector3();

function testBox(
  box: THREE.Box3,
  rect: BoxRect,
  mode: BoxMode,
  hostRect: DOMRect,
  cam: THREE.Camera,
): boolean {
  if (!box || box.isEmpty()) return false;

  let minX = Infinity;
  let minY = Infinity;
  let maxX = -Infinity;
  let maxY = -Infinity;
  let anyVisible = false;

  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) {
    const x = i & 1 ? box.max.x : box.min.x;
    const y = i & 2 ? box.max.y : box.min.y;
    const z = i & 4 ? box.max.z : box.min.z;
    _corner.set(x, y, z).project(cam);

    if (_corner.z < -1 || _corner.z > 1) {
      if (mode === "window") return false;
      continue;
    }

    anyVisible = true;
    const px = (_corner.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * hostRect.width;
    const py = (-_corner.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * hostRect.height;
    if (px < minX) minX = px;
    if (px > maxX) maxX = px;
    if (py < minY) minY = py;
    if (py > maxY) maxY = py;
  }

  if (!anyVisible) return false;

  if (mode === "window") {
    return minX >= rect.x1 && maxX <= rect.x2 && minY >= rect.y1 && maxY <= rect.y2;
  }
  return !(maxX < rect.x1 || minX > rect.x2 || maxY < rect.y1 || minY > rect.y2);
}

async function queryFragments(
  rect: BoxRect,
  mode: BoxMode,
): Promise<Record<string, number[]>> {
  const out: Record<string, number[]> = {};
  const hostRect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;

  for (const [modelId, model] of ctx.fragments.list) {
    let ids: number[] = [];
    try {
      ids = ((await (model as any).getItemsIdsWithGeometry()) as number[]) ?? [];
    } catch {
      continue;
    }
    if (!ids.length) continue;

    const hits: number[] = [];
    const CHUNK = 2000;
    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i += CHUNK) {
      const slice = ids.slice(i, i + CHUNK);
      let boxes: any[] = [];
      try {
        boxes = await getElementWorldBoxes(model, slice, modelId);
      } catch {
        continue;
      }
      for (const wb of boxes) {
        if (testBox(wb.box, rect, mode, hostRect, cam)) hits.push(wb.id);
      }
      await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    }
    if (hits.length) out[modelId] = hits;
  }
  return out;
}

function queryAuthored(rect: BoxRect, mode: BoxMode): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const mod: any = (ctx.features as any).authoring;
  if (!mod?.getElements || !mod?.getElementWorldBox) return out;

  const hostRect = ctx.rightHost.getBoundingClientRect();
  const cam = ctx.world.camera.three;
  const elements = mod.getElements();
  for (const el of elements) {
    const box = mod.getElementWorldBox(el);
    if (!box) continue;
    if (testBox(box, rect, mode, hostRect, cam)) out.push(el.id);
  }
  return out;
}

export async function commitBoxSelection(
  rect: BoxRect,
  mode: BoxMode,
  modifier: "replace" | "add" | "remove" = "replace",
): Promise<void> {
  const [fragHits, authHits] = await Promise.all([
    queryFragments(rect, mode),
    Promise.resolve(queryAuthored(rect, mode)),
  ]);

  if (modifier === "replace") {
    const snap: Record<string, Set<number>> = {};
    for (const [mid, ids] of Object.entries(fragHits)) snap[mid] = new Set(ids);
    selection.replace(snap);
    await selection.applyToHighlighter();
    setSelectedAuthored(authHits, "replace");
  } else if (modifier === "add") {
    for (const [mid, ids] of Object.entries(fragHits)) selection.addMany(mid, ids);
    await selection.applyToHighlighter();
    setSelectedAuthored(authHits, "add");
  } else {
    for (const [mid, ids] of Object.entries(fragHits)) selection.removeMany(mid, ids);
    await selection.applyToHighlighter();
    setSelectedAuthored(authHits, "remove");
  }

  const fragTotal = Object.values(fragHits).reduce((a, s) => a + s.length, 0);
  console.log(
    `[box-select] ${mode} Â· ${modifier} Â· ` +
      `${fragTotal} fragment + ${authHits.length} authored element(s)`,
  );
}

export function setupBoxSelection(): void {
  (ctx.features as any).boxSelection = {
    commit: commitBoxSelection,
    queryAuthored,
    queryFragments,
  };
  console.log("[box-select] ready (owned by navigation)");
}


===== FILE: src/features/lod-manager.ts =====
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";

type Any = Record<string, any>;

let installed = false;
let enabled = true;
let timer: number | null = null;

// openbim-fix:lod-always-on -- LOD culling is permanently disabled.
// Every element stays visible regardless of distance or screen size.
// The flag is a constant so no runtime toggle can re-enable culling.
// The reported state stays ON to keep the toolbar button label stable.
const LOD_CULLING_DISABLED = true;

function c(): Any {
  return (ctx as Any) || (window as Any).__OBS_CTX__ || {};
}

function getAuthoringGroup(): Any | null {
  const cont = c();
  const auth = cont.features?.authoring as Any;

  return (
    auth?.getAuthoredGroup?.() ??
    auth?.group ??
    auth?.root ??
    auth?.three ??
    null
  );
}

function restoreAll(): void {
  const group = getAuthoringGroup();

  if (!group || typeof group.traverse !== "function") {
    return;
  }

  group.traverse((obj: Any) => {
    if (obj?.userData?.__obsLodHidden) {
      obj.visible = true;
      delete obj.userData.__obsLodHidden;
    }
  });
}

function applyLod(): void {
  if (!installed) {
    return;
  }

  // openbim-fix:lod-always-on -- never hide geometry.
  if (LOD_CULLING_DISABLED || !enabled) {
    restoreAll();
    return;
  }

  const cont = c();
  const cam = cont.world?.camera?.three as Any;
  const group = getAuthoringGroup();

  if (!cam || !group || typeof group.traverse !== "function") {
    return;
  }

  try {
    group.updateMatrixWorld?.(true);
    cam.updateMatrixWorld?.(true);
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }

  const cameraPos = new THREE.Vector3().setFromMatrixPosition(cam.matrixWorld);

  group.traverse((obj: Any) => {
    if (!obj?.isMesh || !obj.geometry) {
      return;
    }

    const geo = obj.geometry;

    if (!geo.boundingSphere) {
      try {
        geo.computeBoundingSphere();
      } catch {
        return;
      }
    }

    const sphere = geo.boundingSphere;

    if (!sphere || !Number.isFinite(sphere.radius) || sphere.radius <= 0) {
      return;
    }

    const center = new THREE.Vector3().copy(sphere.center).applyMatrix4(obj.matrixWorld);
    const radius = sphere.radius * Math.max(
      obj.matrixWorld.elements[0] ?? 1,
      obj.matrixWorld.elements[5] ?? 1,
      obj.matrixWorld.elements[10] ?? 1,
    );

    const dist = cameraPos.distanceTo(center);

    let hide = false;

    if (radius < 0.15 && dist > 30) {
      hide = true;
    } else if (radius < 0.6 && dist > 90) {
      hide = true;
    } else if (radius < 2 && dist > 250) {
      hide = true;
    }

    if (hide) {
      if (obj.visible) {
        obj.visible = false;
        obj.userData.__obsLodHidden = true;
      }
    } else if (obj.userData?.__obsLodHidden) {
      obj.visible = true;
      delete obj.userData.__obsLodHidden;
    }
  });
}

export function setLodEnabled(next: boolean): boolean {
  // openbim-fix:lod-always-on -- the toggle is a no-op. Culling is
  // permanently disabled so every detail stays visible.
  void next;
  enabled = true;
  restoreAll();
  return true;
}

export function isLodEnabled(): boolean {
  // openbim-fix:lod-always-on -- always report ON so the toolbar
  // button label never flips. Culling itself is skipped by
  // LOD_CULLING_DISABLED inside applyLod().
  return true;
}

export function setupLodManager(): void {
  if (installed) {
    return;
  }

  installed = true;

  timer = window.setInterval(applyLod, 250);

  (window as Any).__obsLodManager = {
    setEnabled: setLodEnabled,
    isEnabled: isLodEnabled,
    refresh: applyLod,
    restoreAll,
  };

  console.log("[lod-manager] ready");
}

===== FILE: src/features/view-graphics.ts =====
/**
 * View / graphics control layer.
 *
 * Provides persistent presentation presets that act on the current Three.js
 * scene, including loaded Fragments and native authored geometry when their
 * materials expose the standard Three.js graphics properties.
 */
import * as THREE from "three";
import { ctx } from "../core/context";

export type VisualPreset = "shaded" | "shaded-edges" | "wireframe" | "hidden-line";
export type BackgroundPreset = "studio" | "paper" | "night";

let visualPreset: VisualPreset = "shaded";
let sceneTransparency = 0;
let sunEnabled = true;
let background: BackgroundPreset = "studio";

const original = new Map<THREE.Material, {
  opacity: number;
  transparent: boolean;
  wireframe?: boolean;
  depthWrite?: boolean;
  color?: THREE.Color;
}>();
let tracked = false;

function remember(mat: THREE.Material): void {
  if (original.has(mat)) return;
  const m = mat as THREE.Material & { wireframe?: boolean; depthWrite?: boolean; color?: THREE.Color };
  original.set(mat, {
    opacity: m.opacity,
    transparent: m.transparent,
    wireframe: m.wireframe,
    depthWrite: m.depthWrite,
    color: m.color?.clone?.(),
  });
}

function eachMaterial(cb: (mat: THREE.Material) => void): void {
  if (!ctx.world?.scene?.three) return;
  ctx.world.scene.three.traverse((obj: any) => {
    if (!obj?.isMesh || !obj.material) return;
    const mats = Array.isArray(obj.material) ? obj.material : [obj.material];
    for (const mat of mats) {
      if (!mat) continue;
      remember(mat);
      cb(mat as THREE.Material);
    }
  });
  tracked = true;
}

export function setVisualPreset(preset: VisualPreset): void {
  visualPreset = preset;
  eachMaterial((mat) => {
    const m = mat as THREE.Material & { wireframe?: boolean; depthWrite?: boolean };
    const base = original.get(mat);
    if (!base) return;
    if (preset === "wireframe") {
      if (typeof m.wireframe === "boolean") m.wireframe = true;
      m.transparent = true;
      m.opacity = Math.max(0.35, 1 - sceneTransparency / 100);
    } else if (preset === "hidden-line") {
      if (typeof m.wireframe === "boolean") m.wireframe = false;
      m.transparent = true;
      m.opacity = Math.max(0.18, 0.78 - sceneTransparency / 120);
      m.depthWrite = false;
    } else {
      if (typeof m.wireframe === "boolean") m.wireframe = false;
      m.transparent = sceneTransparency > 0 || base.transparent;
      m.opacity = sceneTransparency > 0 ? (1 - sceneTransparency / 100) : base.opacity;
      m.depthWrite = base.depthWrite ?? true;
    }
    m.needsUpdate = true;
  });
}

export function getVisualPreset(): VisualPreset { return visualPreset; }

export function setSceneTransparency(value: number): void {
  sceneTransparency = Math.max(0, Math.min(90, Number(value) || 0));
  setVisualPreset(visualPreset);
}

export function getSceneTransparency(): number { return sceneTransparency; }

export function setSunEnabled(on: boolean): void {
  sunEnabled = !!on;
  if (!ctx.world?.scene?.three) return;
  ctx.world.scene.three.traverse((obj: any) => {
    if (obj?.isDirectionalLight) obj.visible = sunEnabled;
  });
}

export function isSunEnabled(): boolean { return sunEnabled; }

export function setSceneBackground(preset: BackgroundPreset): void {
  background = preset;
  if (!ctx.world?.scene?.three) return;
  const color = preset === "paper" ? 0xf3f4f6 : preset === "night" ? 0x05070b : 0x0f1117;
  ctx.world.scene.three.background = new THREE.Color(color);
}

export function getSceneBackground(): BackgroundPreset { return background; }

export function refreshViewGraphics(): void {
  if (!ctx.world?.scene?.three) return;
  setSceneBackground(background);
  setSunEnabled(sunEnabled);
  if (tracked) setVisualPreset(visualPreset);
}
